  1566	def verify_shortlist(
  1567	    shortlist: list[dict],
  1568	    criteria: str,
  1569	    taste_section: str,
  1570	    feedback_context: str,
  1571	    client,
  1572	) -> list[dict]:
  1573	    """Read each shortlisted article and verify it meets selection criteria.
  1574	
  1575	    Walks through the shortlist in rank order, fetches article content,
  1576	    and asks Claude to verify. Stops once 4 articles pass (or 3 if slot 4
  1577	    wildcard has no good candidate).
  1578	    """
  1579	    section("ARTICLE VERIFICATION")
  1580	    slot_labels = {1: "Healthcare/Biotech", 2: "Finance/Macro", 3: "Tech/AI", 4: "Wildcard"}
  1581	    today = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
  1582	
  1583	    verified = []
  1584	    verification_log = []
  1585	    seen_sources = set()
  1586	    next_slot = 1
  1587	
  1588	    # Articles rejected ONLY because they were offered to the wrong slot, parked
  1589	    # for the slot they actually belong to. Without this, the shortlist is walked
  1590	    # in rank order against whichever slot is currently being filled, so a strong
  1591	    # Slot-1 story that happens to surface while Slot 3 is open is rejected as
  1592	    # off-theme and BURNED — it can never fill the slot it was right for.
  1593	    # Measured on artifacts/verification_log.json since 2026-05-01: 89 of 411
  1594	    # failures (21.7%) were this, and the verifier's own reasons named the right
  1595	    # slot. Casualties included "Sun Pharma signs $11.75B Organon buyout" and a
  1596	    # Biogen tau Phase 2 readout — both rejected FOR SLOT 3.
  1597	    # Each entry keeps its already-extracted text: extraction is the expensive and
  1598	    # fragile half, so a retry costs one cheap verification call and no refetch.
  1599	    parked: dict[int, list[dict]] = {}
  1600	    retried: set[str] = set()   # URLs already given a second chance — retry once, never loop
  1601	
  1602	    queue = list(shortlist)
  1603	    while next_slot <= 4:
  1604	        # Anything parked for the slot now being filled goes first: it was held
  1605	        # precisely because it belongs here, so it outranks a fresh candidate.
  1606	        pending = parked.pop(next_slot, [])
  1607	        if pending:
  1608	            candidate = pending.pop(0)
  1609	            if pending:
  1610	                parked[next_slot] = pending
  1611	        elif queue:
  1612	            candidate = queue.pop(0)
  1613	        else:
  1614	            # Nothing fresh and nothing parked for this slot. Advance rather than
  1615	            # stop, so a candidate parked for a LATER slot still gets its turn —
  1616	            # the queue emptying must not strand the thing we deliberately held.
  1617	            next_slot += 1
  1618	            continue
  1619	
  1620	        url = (candidate.get("url") or "").strip()
  1621	        headline = candidate.get("headline", "Untitled")
  1622	        source = candidate.get("source", "Unknown")
  1623	
  1624	        # Skip duplicate sources
  1625	        if source.casefold() in seen_sources:
  1626	            print(f"  Skipping {headline[:50]} — duplicate source {source}")
  1627	            continue
  1628	
  1629	        if not url or not re.match(r"^https?://", url):
  1630	            print(f"  Skipping {headline[:50]} — invalid URL")
  1631	            continue
  1632	
  1633	        print(f"\n  Reading #{candidate.get('rank', '?')}: {headline[:60]}")
  1634	        print(f"    URL: {url}")
  1635	
  1636	        # A parked candidate already paid for extraction; reuse it.
  1637	        cached_text = candidate.get("_article_text")
  1638	        if cached_text:
  1639	            article_text, extraction_tier = cached_text, candidate.get("_extraction_tier")
  1640	        else:
  1641	            article_text, extraction_tier = fetch_article_text(url)
  1642	        snippet_only = bool(candidate.get("_snippet_only"))
  1643	        if not article_text:
  1644	            # Fall back to newsletter snippet for paywalled/unfetchable articles
  1645	            snippet = candidate.get("summary", "")
  1646	            why = candidate.get("why_it_matters", "")
  1647	            fallback = f"Headline: {headline}\nSource: {source}\nSummary: {snippet}\nWhy it matters: {why}"
  1648	            if len(snippet) > 50:
  1649	                print(f"    Could not fetch article — using newsletter snippet for lighter verification")
  1650	                article_text = fallback
  1651	                snippet_only = True
  1652	                extraction_tier = "snippet_fallback"
  1653	            else:
  1654	                print(f"    Could not extract article text and no snippet — skipping")
  1655	                verification_log.append({
  1656	                    "date": today, "headline": headline, "source": source,
  1657	                    "url": url, "extraction_tier": "none",
  1658	                    "passed": False, "reason": "Could not fetch and no snippet",
  1659	                })
  1660	                continue
  1661	
  1662	        if not snippet_only:
  1663	            print(f"    Fetched {len(article_text)} chars via {extraction_tier}")
  1664	
  1665	        # Ask Claude to verify this article
  1666	        snippet_caveat = ""
  1667	        if snippet_only:
  1668	            snippet_caveat = """
  1669	NOTE: The full article could not be fetched (likely paywalled). You are evaluating based on
  1670	the headline and newsletter summary only. Apply a lighter bar — accept if the topic and source
  1671	are strong and the summary suggests substantive content. Reject only if the topic clearly
  1672	doesn't fit the criteria or seems thin/generic based on what's available."""
  1673	
  1674	        verify_prompt = f"""You are verifying whether an article meets selection criteria for a daily digest.{snippet_caveat}
  1675	
  1676	SELECTION CRITERIA:
  1677	{criteria}
  1678	{taste_section}
  1679	{feedback_context}
  1680	
  1681	TARGET SLOT: Slot {next_slot} — {slot_labels.get(next_slot, 'General')}
  1682	
  1683	ARTICLE HEADLINE: {headline}
  1684	ARTICLE SOURCE: {source}
  1685	SHORTLIST SUMMARY: {candidate.get('summary', '')}
  1686	SHORTLIST WHY IT MATTERS: {candidate.get('why_it_matters', '')}
  1687	
  1688	FULL ARTICLE TEXT:
  1689	{article_text}
  1690	
  1691	Based on the actual article content (not just the headline), evaluate:
  1692	1. Does this article have real substance and depth, or is it thin/generic?
  1693	2. Does it match the selection criteria and the target slot theme?
  1694	3. Is the shortlist summary accurate to what the article actually says?
  1695	
  1696	Return ONLY valid JSON with these keys:
  1697	- "pass": true or false
  1698	- "reason": one sentence explaining your verdict
  1699	- "best_slot": if the article is substantive but belongs to a DIFFERENT slot than the target, the slot number it fits best (1 Healthcare/Biotech, 2 Finance/Macro, 3 Tech/AI, 4 Wildcard). Use null when it fits the target slot, or when it is too thin/generic for any slot. Judge this on topic fit alone — an article rejected for lacking substance has no best slot.
  1700	- "summary": an accurate 2-3 sentence summary based on the actual content (rewrite if the original was inaccurate)
  1701	- "why_it_matters": why this matters for the reader, based on actual content
  1702	- "reading_time": estimated minutes to read (e.g. "4 min")
  1703	"""
  1704	
  1705	        try:
  1706	            verify_resp = client.messages.create(
  1707	                model="claude-sonnet-4-6",
  1708	                max_tokens=1024,
  1709	                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": verify_prompt}],
  1710	            )
  1711	            verify_text = ""
  1712	            for block in verify_resp.content:
  1713	                if block.type == "text":
  1714	                    verify_text += block.text
  1715	
  1716	            json_match = re.search(r'\{.*\}', verify_text, re.DOTALL)
  1717	            if not json_match:
  1718	                print(f"    Could not parse verification response — skipping")
  1719	                continue
  1720	
  1721	            verdict = json.loads(json_match.group())
  1722	        except Exception as e:
  1723	            print(f"    Verification call failed: {e} — skipping")
  1724	            continue
  1725	
  1726	        passed = bool(verdict.get("pass"))
  1727	        reason = verdict.get("reason", "")
  1728	        verification_log.append({
  1729	            "date": today, "headline": headline, "source": source,
  1730	            "url": url, "extraction_tier": extraction_tier,
  1731	            "passed": passed, "reason": reason,
  1732	        })
  1733	
  1734	        if passed:
  1735	            print(f"    PASS: {reason}")
  1736	            verified.append({
  1737	                "article_id": article_id_for(url, source),
  1738	                "headline": headline.strip(),
  1739	                "source": source.strip(),
  1740	                "url": url,
  1741	                "slot": next_slot,
  1742	                "summary": verdict.get("summary", candidate.get("summary", "")).strip(),
  1743	                "why_it_matters": verdict.get("why_it_matters", candidate.get("why_it_matters", "")).strip(),
  1744	                "signal_tags": [str(t).strip() for t in candidate.get("signal_tags", []) if str(t).strip()],
  1745	                "reading_time": verdict.get("reading_time", candidate.get("reading_time", "N/A")),
  1746	                # Carry the snippet-only status downstream so every delivered
  1747	                # surface can mark a paywalled item as verified-on-summary rather
  1748	                # than full-text (NO SILENT FALLBACKS — the reader must see it).
  1749	                "snippet_only": snippet_only,
  1750	            })
  1751	            seen_sources.add(source.casefold())
  1752	            next_slot += 1
  1753	        else:
  1754	            print(f"    FAIL: {reason}")
  1755	            # Rejected only on slot fit? Hold it for the slot it belongs to
  1756	            # rather than discarding a good article for arriving out of order.
  1757	            try:
  1758	                best = int(verdict.get("best_slot") or 0)
  1759	            except (TypeError, ValueError):
  1760	                best = 0
  1761	            if (best in (1, 2, 3, 4) and best != next_slot and best >= next_slot
  1762	                    and url not in retried):
  1763	                retried.add(url)
  1764	                parked.setdefault(best, []).append({
  1765	                    **candidate,
  1766	                    "_article_text": article_text,
  1767	                    "_extraction_tier": extraction_tier,
  1768	                    "_snippet_only": snippet_only,
  1769	                })
  1770	                print(f"    -> held for slot {best}")
  1771	
  1772	    # Persist verification log for weekly reporting
  1773	    log_path = Path("artifacts/verification_log.json")
  1774	    existing_log = []
  1775	    if log_path.exists():
  1776	        try:
  1777	            existing_log = json.loads(log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  1778	        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
  1779	            pass
  1780	    existing_log.extend(verification_log)
  1781	    os.makedirs("artifacts", exist_ok=True)
  1782	    log_path.write_text(json.dumps(existing_log, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
  1783	
  1784	    # Accept 3 articles if we couldn't fill the wildcard slot
  1785	    if len(verified) >= 3:
  1786	        return verified
  1787	    return verified

  1820	                    'border-radius: 4px; padding: 4px 10px; color: #eee; font-size: 13px; '

  1826	            html += f"""

  1828	  <h2 style="margin: 0 0 8px 0;">{emoji} <a href="{a.get('url', '#')}" style="color: #0fbcf9; text-decoration: none;">{a.get('headline', 'Untitled')}</a></h2>
  1829	  <p style="color: #a8a8b3; margin: 4px 0; font-size: 13px;">{a.get('source', '')} · Slot {slot} · ⏱ {a.get('reading_time', 'N/A')} read{' · 🔒 snippet-only (paywalled, verified on summary)' if a.get('snippet_only') else ''}</p>
  1830	  <p style="margin: 8px 0;">{a.get('summary', '')}</p>
  1831	  <p style="color: #e94560; font-style: italic; margin: 8px 0;">💡 {a.get('why_it_matters', '')}</p>
  1832	  <p style="margin: 8px 0;">{feedback_html}</p>
  1833	  <p style="color: #666; font-size: 11px;">Signals: {', '.join(a.get('signal_tags', []))} · ⏱ {a.get('reading_time', 'N/A')} read</p>
  1834	</div>
  1835	"""
  1836	        if triage_queue:
  1837	            html += """
  1838	<div style="background: #1a1a2e; border-top: 2px solid #333; margin-top: 24px; padding-top: 16px;">
  1839	  <h3 style="color: #a8a8b3; margin: 0 0 4px 0;">Also considered</h3>
  1840	  <p style="color: #666; font-size: 11px; margin: 0 0 12px 0;">Reply to rate: <span style="color: #0fbcf9;">[slot#] [score 1-3]</span> — e.g. <span style="color: #0fbcf9;">5 3</span> = slot 5, strong pick; <span style="color: #0fbcf9;">7 1 not relevant</span> = slot 7, miss</p>
  1841	"""
  1842	            for i, candidate in enumerate(triage_queue[:10]):
  1843	                slot_num = i + 5
  1844	                headline = candidate.get("headline", "Untitled")
  1845	                url = candidate.get("primary_url", "#")
  1846	                source = candidate.get("source_name", "")
  1847	                html += f'  <p style="margin: 6px 0; font-size: 13px;"><span style="color: #a8a8b3; font-size: 11px; margin-right: 6px;">#{slot_num}</span><a href="{url}" style="color: #0fbcf9; text-decoration: none;">{headline}</a> <span style="color: #666;">— {source}</span></p>\n'
  1848	            html += "</div>\n"
  1849	
  1850	        if always_read:
  1851	            html += """

  1853	  <h3 style="color: #e94560; margin: 0 0 8px 0;">📖 Always read</h3>

  1873	            html += "</div>\n"

  1881	<a href="https://github.com/jroypeterson/daily-reads/issues/new?labels=taste&title=Taste%3A+&body=Paste+URL+here%0A%0AWhy+I+liked+it%3A+" style="color: #0fbcf9;">📎 Submit an article</a></p>
  1882	<p style="color: #666; font-size: 12px;">📬 Found something great? Forward it to <a href="mailto:jroypeterson+taste@gmail.com" style="color: #0fbcf9;">jroypeterson+taste@gmail.com</a> to train my taste. Add "Why I liked it:" in the body for extra signal.</p>
  1883	</body></html>"""
  1884	
  1885	        msg = MIMEText(html, "html")
  1886	        msg["to"] = "jroypeterson@gmail.com"
  1887	        msg["subject"] = f"[ClaudeFin] daily-reads — 📰 Daily digest ({today})"
  1888	        raw = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(msg.as_bytes()).decode()
  1889	
  1890	        service.users().messages().send(

  1893	        print("Email sent to jasonrpeterson@gmail.com")
  1894	    except Exception as e:

  1898	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1899	# [DELIVERY: SLACK]

  1901	
  1902	SLACK_SECTION_HARD_LIMIT = 2990  # Slack rejects sections > 3000 chars
  1903	
  1904	
  1905	def _split_oversized_section_blocks(blocks: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
  1906	    """Belt-and-suspenders against Slack's 3000-char-per-section limit.
  1907	
  1908	    The triage / always-read / substack sections each chunk themselves,
  1909	    but any new section type (or one that gets refactored later) can
  1910	    silently exceed the limit and trip invalid_blocks. This pass walks
  1911	    the assembled payload and splits any oversized section on newline
  1912	    boundaries. Other block types pass through unchanged.
  1913	    """
  1914	    out: list[dict] = []
  1915	    for block in blocks:
  1916	        if block.get("type") != "section":
  1917	            out.append(block)
  1918	            continue
  1919	        text_obj = block.get("text") or {}
  1920	        text = text_obj.get("text", "")
  1921	        if len(text) <= SLACK_SECTION_HARD_LIMIT:
  1922	            out.append(block)
  1923	            continue
  1924	        text_type = text_obj.get("type", "mrkdwn")
  1925	        chunks: list[str] = []
  1926	        current = ""
  1927	        for line in text.split("\n"):
  1928	            if len(line) > SLACK_SECTION_HARD_LIMIT:
  1929	                line = line[: SLACK_SECTION_HARD_LIMIT - 4] + "..."
  1930	            candidate = (current + "\n" + line) if current else line
  1931	            if len(candidate) > SLACK_SECTION_HARD_LIMIT and current:
  1932	                chunks.append(current)
  1933	                current = line
  1934	            else:
  1935	                current = candidate
  1936	        if current:
  1937	            chunks.append(current)
  1938	        print(f"Auto-split oversized section ({len(text)} chars) into {len(chunks)} chunks")
  1939	        for chunk in chunks:
  1940	            out.append({"type": "section", "text": {"type": text_type, "text": chunk}})
  1941	    return out
  1942	
  1943	
  1944	def _alert_operator_slack(message: str) -> None:
  1945	    """Post a one-line failure alert to #status-reports so the digest
  1946	    can't fail silently for days. Always records the failure as a
  1947	    partial-run signal so the end-of-run heartbeat reflects it. Only
  1948	    posts the standalone alert when the operator webhook is a separate
  1949	    channel from the digest webhook — otherwise the alert would land in
  1950	    the same broken pipe.
  1951	    """
  1952	    _RUN_STATE["partial_reasons"].append(f"Slack digest delivery failed: {message[:200]}")
  1953	
  1954	    operator_url = os.environ.get("SLACK_WEBHOOK_STATUS_REPORTS")
  1955	    daily_reads_url = os.environ.get("SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL_DAILY_READS")
  1956	    if not operator_url or operator_url == daily_reads_url:
  1957	        return
  1958	    try:
  1959	        requests.post(
  1960	            operator_url,
  1961	            json={
  1962	                "blocks": [
  1963	                    {
  1964	                        "type": "section",
  1965	                        "text": {
  1966	                            "type": "mrkdwn",
  1967	                            "text": f":warning: *Daily Reads digest delivery failed*\n{message[:500]}",
  1968	                        },
  1969	                    }
  1970	                ],
  1971	                "text": f"Daily Reads digest delivery failed: {message[:500]}",
  1972	            },
  1973	            timeout=10,
  1974	        )
  1975	    except Exception as e:
  1976	        print(f"Operator alert post also failed: {e}")
  1977	
  1978	
  1979	def deliver_slack(articles: list[dict], triage_queue: list[dict] | None = None, always_read: list[dict] | None = None, substack_items: list[dict] | None = None, journal_watch: list[dict] | None = None):
  1980	    section("DELIVERY: SLACK")
  1981	    # The daily digest posts to its own #daily-reads channel when its
  1982	    # dedicated webhook is configured. Fall back to the #status-reports
  1983	    # webhook so a missing secret doesn't break delivery (cross-channel
  1984	    # leak is a smaller blast radius than a silent dropped digest).
  1985	    webhook_url = (
  1986	        os.environ.get("SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL_DAILY_READS")
  1987	        or os.environ.get("SLACK_WEBHOOK_STATUS_REPORTS")
  1988	    )
  1989	    if not webhook_url:
  1990	        print("No Slack webhook set (checked SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL_DAILY_READS, SLACK_WEBHOOK_STATUS_REPORTS) — skipping Slack delivery")
  1991	        return
  1992	
  1993	    today = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
  1994	    slot_emojis = {1: ":dna:", 2: ":chart_with_upwards_trend:", 3: ":robot_face:", 4: ":cyclone:"}
  1995	
  1996	    blocks = [
  1997	        {"type": "header", "text": {"type": "plain_text", "text": f"📰 Daily Reads — {today}"}},
  1998	        {"type": "divider"},
  1999	    ]
  2000	
  2001	    for a in articles:
  2002	        slot = a.get("slot", 0)
  2003	        emoji = slot_emojis.get(slot, ":pushpin:")
  2004	        strong_url = slack_mailto_feedback_url(today, slot, 3)
  2005	        fine_url = slack_mailto_feedback_url(today, slot, 2)
  2006	        miss_url = slack_mailto_feedback_url(today, slot, 1)
  2007	        blocks.append({
  2008	            "type": "section",
  2009	            "text": {
  2010	                "type": "mrkdwn",
  2011	                "text": (
  2012	                    f"{emoji} *<{a.get('url', '#')}|{a.get('headline', 'Untitled')}>*\n"
  2013	                    f"_{a.get('source', '')} · Slot {slot} · :timer_clock: {a.get('reading_time', 'N/A')} read_"
  2014	                    f"{'  :lock: _snippet-only — paywalled, verified on summary_' if a.get('snippet_only') else ''}\n\n"
  2015	                    f"{a.get('summary', '')}\n\n"
  2016	                    f"💡 _{a.get('why_it_matters', '')}_\n\n"
  2017	                    f"<{strong_url}|:thumbsup: Strong pick>  "
  2018	                    f"<{fine_url}|:ok_hand: Fine>  "
  2019	                    f"<{miss_url}|:thumbsdown: Miss>"
  2020	                ),
  2021	            },
  2022	        })
  2023	        blocks.append({"type": "divider"})
  2024	
  2025	    if triage_queue:
  2026	        # Slack section text is capped at 3000 chars. Tracking-redirect URLs
  2027	        # (e.g. BioSpace marketing links) can be 600+ chars each, so chunk
  2028	        # the list across multiple section blocks to stay under the limit.
  2029	        triage_lines = [
  2030	            f"`#{i + 5}` <{c.get('primary_url', '#')}|{c.get('headline', 'Untitled')}> — {c.get('source_name', '')}"
  2031	            for i, c in enumerate(triage_queue[:10])
  2032	        ]
  2033	        header = "*Also considered*\n_Reply to rate: `[slot#] [score 1-3]` — 3 = strong pick, 2 = fine, 1 = miss. e.g. `5 3` or `7 1 not relevant`_"
  2034	        SLACK_SECTION_LIMIT = 2500  # leave headroom under the 3000 cap
  2035	        chunks: list[str] = []
  2036	        current = header
  2037	        for line in triage_lines:
  2038	            candidate = current + "\n" + line
  2039	            if len(candidate) > SLACK_SECTION_LIMIT and current:
  2040	                chunks.append(current)
  2041	                current = line
  2042	            else:
  2043	                current = candidate
  2044	        if current:
  2045	            chunks.append(current)
  2046	        for chunk in chunks:
  2047	            blocks.append({
  2048	                "type": "section",
  2049	                # Hard cap at 2990 as a final safety net against any single
  2050	                # chunk that slipped past the limit (e.g. one line > limit).
  2051	                "text": {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": chunk[:2990]},
  2052	            })
  2053	
  2054	    if journal_watch:
  2055	        # Journals — one line per issue + indented must-read picks (JP 2026-07-06).
  2056	        journal_lines = [":microscope: *Journals — this week's issues*"]

  2392	def deliver_reader(articles: list[dict], always_read: list[dict] | None = None,
  2393	                   state: dict | None = None, today: str | None = None):
  2394	    """Push the day's top picks + always-read items into Readwise Reader so
  2395	    they're queued to read in the app.
  2396	
  2397	    Uses the Readwise REST API (https://readwise.io/api/v3/save/), NOT the
  2398	    interactive MCP — this runs in the GH Actions cloud cron, which can't reach
  2399	    interactive MCP servers. Auth is the static personal token in READWISE_TOKEN
  2400	    (header `Authorization: Token <token>`), distinct from the OAuth/Bearer MCP.
  2401	
  2402	    **READER DOES NOT DEDUPE BY URL — this docstring claimed it did, and it is
  2403	    false.** Measured 2026-09-06 with a controlled probe: saving one URL twice
  2404	    returns two different document ids, for a real https URL and a synthetic one
  2405	    alike. The 200-vs-201 reading was wrong.
  2406	
  2407	    Nothing has actually duplicated yet: a full sweep the same day found 0
  2408	    repeated source_urls across the 316 daily-reads documents. The hole is real
  2409	    but latent, because the delivered-id ledger excludes on a ROLLING WINDOW and
  2410	    has so far covered it. It cannot be relied on to: an always-read source that
  2411	    keeps listing the same URL (a quarterly letter sitting on a fund's page for
  2412	    months) ages out of the window and would be pushed again. `reader_pushed`
  2413	    below is the permanent record that closes it.
  2414	
  2415	    Both lists land in `location` (default 'later'); items are tagged so they're
  2416	    filterable in Reader ('daily-reads' + 'top-pick'/'always-read').
  2417	    """
  2418	    section("DELIVERY: READWISE READER")
  2419	    token = os.environ.get("READWISE_TOKEN")
  2420	    if not token:
  2421	        print("READWISE_TOKEN not configured — skipping Reader push.")
  2422	        return
  2423	
  2424	    location = (os.environ.get("READWISE_READER_LOCATION") or "later").strip()
  2425	    headers = {
  2426	        "Authorization": f"Token {token}",
  2427	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
  2428	    }
  2429	
  2430	    # Build the save list. Top picks and always-read use different field names
  2431	    # for their URL/headline (see build_always_read), so normalise here.
  2432	    saves: list[dict] = []
  2433	    for a in (articles or []):
  2434	        url = (a.get("url") or "").strip()
  2435	        if not url:
  2436	            continue
  2437	        tags = ["daily-reads", "top-pick"]
  2438	        if a.get("snippet_only"):
  2439	            # Mark paywalled/verified-on-summary picks so they're filterable in Reader.
  2440	            tags.append("snippet-only")
  2441	        payload = {
  2442	            "url": url,
  2443	            "title": a.get("headline") or "Untitled",
  2444	            "location": location,
  2445	            "category": "article",
  2446	            "saved_using": "daily-reads",
  2447	            "tags": tags,
  2448	        }
  2449	        summary = a.get("summary") or ""
  2450	        if summary:
  2451	            payload["summary"] = summary
  2452	        saves.append(payload)
  2453	
  2454	    for item in (always_read or []):
  2455	        url = (item.get("primary_url") or "").strip()
  2456	        if not url:
  2457	            continue
  2458	        saves.append({
  2459	            "url": url,
  2460	            "title": item.get("headline") or item.get("subject") or "Untitled",
  2461	            "location": location,
  2462	            "category": "article",
  2463	            "saved_using": "daily-reads",
  2464	            "tags": ["daily-reads", "always-read"],
  2465	        })
  2466	
  2467	    # Drop anything already in Reader. This is the ONLY thing preventing
  2468	    # duplicates: Reader does not dedupe by URL, and the rolling delivered-id
  2469	    # window ages out exactly the long-lived always-read URLs that recur.
  2470	    if state is not None:
  2471	        before = len(saves)
  2472	        saves = [p for p in saves if not reader_already_pushed(state, p["url"])]
  2473	        if before != len(saves):
  2474	            print(f"  Skipped {before - len(saves)} URL(s) already in Reader.")
  2475	
  2476	    if not saves:
  2477	        print("No URLs to push to Reader.")
  2478	        return
  2479	
  2480	    saved = 0
  2481	    bad_token = False
  2482	    failures = 0
  2483	    for payload in saves:
  2484	        try:
  2485	            resp = requests.post(
  2486	                "https://readwise.io/api/v3/save/",
  2487	                headers=headers,
  2488	                json=payload,
  2489	                timeout=30,
  2490	            )
  2491	        except requests.RequestException as exc:
  2492	            failures += 1
  2493	            print(f"  ✗ Network error: {payload['title']} — {exc}")
  2494	            continue
  2495	
  2496	        # Reader rate limit (default 20/min) returns 429 + Retry-After. Volume
  2497	        # is small (a handful of picks), so a single bounded retry is enough.
  2498	        if resp.status_code == 429:
  2499	            wait = int(resp.headers.get("Retry-After", "5") or "5")
  2500	            print(f"  … 429 rate-limited, waiting {wait}s and retrying once")
  2501	            time.sleep(min(wait, 60))
  2502	            try:
  2503	                resp = requests.post(
  2504	                    "https://readwise.io/api/v3/save/",
  2505	                    headers=headers,
  2506	                    json=payload,
  2507	                    timeout=30,
  2508	                )
  2509	            except requests.RequestException as exc:
  2510	                failures += 1
  2511	                print(f"  ✗ Network error on retry: {payload['title']} — {exc}")
  2512	                continue
  2513	
  2514	        if resp.status_code in (200, 201):
  2515	            saved += 1
  2516	            # NOT named `state`: that name holds the ledger in this scope now,
  2517	            # and rebinding it here would silently disable the dedupe after the
  2518	            # first successful save — the bug would look fixed and not be.
  2519	            outcome = "created" if resp.status_code == 201 else "exists"
  2520	            if state is not None:
  2521	                record_reader_push(state, payload["url"], today or "")
  2522	            print(f"  ✓ ({outcome}) {payload['title']}")
  2523	        elif resp.status_code in (401, 403):
  2524	            bad_token = True
  2525	            print(f"  ✗ {resp.status_code} — READWISE_TOKEN rejected.")
  2526	            break
  2527	        else:
  2528	            failures += 1
  2529	            print(f"  ✗ Failed ({resp.status_code}): {payload['title']}")
  2530	            print(f"    {resp.text[:200]}")
  2531	
  2532	    if bad_token:
  2533	        _RUN_STATE["partial_reasons"].append("Readwise token rejected — push to Reader skipped")
  2534	        slack_url = os.environ.get("SLACK_WEBHOOK_STATUS_REPORTS")
  2535	        if slack_url:
  2536	            alert = (
  2537	                ":warning: *Readwise token rejected* — Daily Reads can't push to Reader. "
  2538	                "Mint a new static token at https://readwise.io/access_token, then update "
  2539	                "the `READWISE_TOKEN` GitHub secret (and `daily-reads/.env` locally)."
  2540	            )
  2541	            try:
  2542	                requests.post(slack_url, json={
  2543	                    "blocks": [{"type": "section", "text": {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": alert}}],
  2544	                    "text": "Readwise token rejected — push skipped",
  2545	                }, timeout=15)
  2546	            except requests.RequestException:
  2547	                pass
  2548	    elif failures:
  2549	        _RUN_STATE["partial_reasons"].append(
  2550	            f"Readwise Reader push: {failures}/{len(saves)} items failed to save"
  2551	        )
  2552	
  2553	    print(f"\nSaved {saved}/{len(saves)} items to Readwise Reader (location={location}).")
  2554

  2572	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2573	# [CRITERIA REWRITE]
  2574	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2575	
  2576	def rewrite_criteria(feedback: list[dict]):
  2577	    """Generate a proposed criteria update and notify for review."""
  2578	    section("CRITERIA REWRITE")
  2579	    state = load_criteria_state()
  2580	    pending = state.get("pending")
  2581	    if pending and pending.get("status") == "pending":
  2582	        print(f"Pending criteria proposal already exists: {pending.get('proposal_id')}")
  2583	        return
  2584	
  2585	    print("Generating proposed criteria update based on feedback...")
  2586	
  2587	    with open("selection_criteria.md", "r") as f:
  2588	        current = f.read()
  2589	
  2590	    prior_proposal = ""
  2591	    modification_note = ""
  2592	    revision = 1
  2593	    trigger = "7+ days of feedback accumulated"
  2594	    if pending and pending.get("status") == "modification_requested":
  2595	        revision = int(pending.get("revision", 1)) + 1
  2596	        modification_note = pending.get("modification_note", "").strip()
  2597	        trigger = "user requested modifications to prior proposal"
  2598	        try:
  2599	            with open(PROPOSED_CRITERIA_PATH, "r") as f:
  2600	                prior_proposal = f.read()
  2601	        except FileNotFoundError:
  2602	            prior_proposal = ""
  2603	
  2604	    client = anthropic.Anthropic()
  2605	    response = client.messages.create(
  2606	        model="claude-sonnet-4-6",
  2607	        max_tokens=4096,
  2608	        messages=[{
  2609	            "role": "user",
  2610	            "content": f"""You are refining article selection criteria for a daily newsletter digest.
  2611	
  2612	Current criteria:
  2613	{current}
  2614	
  2615	Accumulated feedback (each entry has date, slot, score 1-3, and optional note):
  2616	{json.dumps(feedback, indent=2)}
  2617	
  2618	Current proposed criteria (if revising an earlier proposal):
  2619	{prior_proposal or "(none)"}
  2620	
  2621	Requested modifications from the user:
  2622	{modification_note or "(none)"}
  2623	
  2624	Analyze the feedback patterns:
  2625	- High scores (3): What patterns should be reinforced?
  2626	- Neutral scores (2): What patterns are acceptable but not distinctive?
  2627	- Low scores (1): What patterns should be reduced?
  2628	
  2629	Return ONLY valid JSON with this schema:
  2630	{{
  2631	  "summary": ["short bullet 1", "short bullet 2", "short bullet 3"],
  2632	  "proposed_markdown": "# Article Selection Criteria\\n..."
  2633	}}
  2634	
  2635	The markdown should keep the same general structure as the current criteria file.
  2636	The summary should be concise and describe the highest-impact changes."""
  2637	        }],
  2638	    )
  2639	
  2640	    payload = None
  2641	    for block in response.content:
  2642	        if block.type != "text":
  2643	            continue
  2644	        text = block.text.strip()
  2645	        json_match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, re.DOTALL)
  2646	        if not json_match:
  2647	            continue
  2648	        try:
  2649	            payload = json.loads(json_match.group())
  2650	            break
  2651	        except json.JSONDecodeError:
  2652	            continue
  2653	
  2654	    if not payload:
  2655	        print("WARNING: could not parse criteria proposal response")
  2656	        return
  2657	
  2658	    proposed_markdown = str(payload.get("proposed_markdown", "")).strip()
  2659	    summary = payload.get("summary", [])
  2660	    if not proposed_markdown or not isinstance(summary, list):
  2661	        print("WARNING: criteria proposal response missing required fields")
  2662	        return
  2663	    diff_lines = build_criteria_diff_lines(current, proposed_markdown)
  2664	
  2665	    with open(PROPOSED_CRITERIA_PATH, "w") as f:
  2666	        f.write(proposed_markdown + "\n")
  2667	
  2668	    proposal_id = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d") + f"-r{revision}"
  2669	    if pending:
  2670	        state["history"].append({
  2671	            **pending,
  2672	            "resolved_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
  2673	            "resolution": "superseded" if pending.get("status") == "modification_requested" else pending.get("status"),
  2674	        })
  2675	
  2676	    state["pending"] = {
  2677	        "proposal_id": proposal_id,
  2678	        "created_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
  2679	        "status": "pending",
  2680	        "revision": revision,
  2681	        "trigger": trigger,
  2682	        "summary": [str(item).strip() for item in summary if str(item).strip()],
  2683	        "diff_lines": diff_lines,
  2684	        "modification_note": "",
  2685	    }
  2686	    save_criteria_state(state)
  2687	    print(f"Proposed criteria update saved to {PROPOSED_CRITERIA_PATH}")
  2688	    notify_criteria_update(state["pending"])
  2689

  2766	def _build_run_link() -> str:
  2767	    server = os.environ.get("GITHUB_SERVER_URL")
  2768	    repo = os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY")
  2769	    run_id = os.environ.get("GITHUB_RUN_ID")
  2770	    if server and repo and run_id:
  2771	        return f"<{server}/{repo}/actions/runs/{run_id}|GH Actions run>"
  2772	    return ""
  2773	
  2774	
  2775	def _next_expected_daily(now: datetime) -> str:
  2776	    # Daily run at 12:00 UTC; if we're past today's slot, point to tomorrow.
  2777	    target = now.replace(hour=12, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
  2778	    if now >= target:
  2779	        target = target + timedelta(days=1)
  2780	    return target.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")
  2781	
  2782	
  2783	def _read_url_validation_warnings(today: str) -> list[str]:
  2784	    """Pull today's URL liveness drop counts from the validation log."""
  2785	    log = load_json("artifacts/url_validation_log.json", [])
  2786	    if not isinstance(log, list):
  2787	        return []
  2788	    todays = [e for e in log if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("date") == today]
  2789	    if not todays:
  2790	        return []
  2791	    entry = todays[-1]
  2792	    broken = entry.get("broken", {}) or {}
  2793	    parts: list[str] = []
  2794	    if broken.get("article_warnings"):
  2795	        parts.append(
  2796	            f"{broken['article_warnings']} main-slot URL(s) shipped broken "
  2797	            f"(no substitute available)"
  2798	        )
  2799	    if broken.get("article_substituted"):
  2800	        parts.append(
  2801	            f"{broken['article_substituted']} main-slot URL(s) were broken and "
  2802	            f"replaced from the also-considered queue"
  2803	        )
  2804	    triage_n = broken.get("triage_dropped", 0) + broken.get("always_read_dropped", 0) + broken.get("substack_dropped", 0)
  2805	    if triage_n:
  2806	        parts.append(f"{triage_n} non-slot URL(s) dropped (broken)")
  2807	    return parts
  2808

  2810	def _post_run_heartbeat(
  2811	    *,
  2812	    start_time: datetime,

  2814	    counters: list[str],
  2815	    artifacts: list[str],

  2818	) -> None:
  2819	    end_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
  2820	    cycle_date = start_time.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
  2821	    try:
  2822	        post_health_to_slack(Heartbeat(
  2823	            project="daily-reads",
  2824	            status=status,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
  2825	            cycle=f"{cycle_date} daily",
  2826	            start_time=start_time,
  2827	            end_time=end_time,

  2831	            warnings=warnings,

  2835	    except Exception as e:

  2837	        print(f"\n[health/v1] heartbeat post failed: {e}")
  2838	        raise

  2841	def delivered_candidate_ids(articles: list[dict], structured: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
  2842	    """Map each delivered article back to the id used for cross-run exclusion.
  2843	
  2844	    Exclusion keys on ``candidate_id`` (= article_id_for(primary_url, source_name)),
  2845	    but a delivered article carries the url/source Claude *returned*, which can
  2841	def delivered_candidate_ids(articles: list[dict], structured: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
  2842	    """Map each delivered article back to the id used for cross-run exclusion.
  2843	
  2844	    Exclusion keys on ``candidate_id`` (= article_id_for(primary_url, source_name)),
  2845	    but a delivered article carries the url/source Claude *returned*, which can
  2846	    diverge from the candidate (URL rewrite, relabelled source). Recording
  2847	    article_id_for(url, source) would then silently fail to match next run's
  2848	    candidate_id and the dedup would no-op. So we resolve each article back to
  2849	    its originating candidate by normalized URL (source-qualified first) and
  2850	    record that candidate's id — guaranteeing record-key ≡ exclude-key. Only
  2851	    when no candidate matches (Claude invented a URL) do we fall back to the
  2852	    article's own id."""
  2853	    by_url_source: dict[tuple[str, str], str] = {}
  2854	    by_url: dict[str, str] = {}
  2855	    for c in structured:
  2856	        u = normalize_url(c.get("primary_url", ""))
  2857	        if not u:
  2858	            continue
  2859	        by_url.setdefault(u, c["candidate_id"])
  2860	        by_url_source.setdefault((u, str(c.get("source_name", "")).casefold()), c["candidate_id"])
  2861	
  2862	    ids: list[str] = []
  2863	    for a in articles:
  2864	        u = normalize_url(a.get("url", ""))
  2865	        src = str(a.get("source", "")).casefold()
  2866	        cid = by_url_source.get((u, src)) or by_url.get(u)
  2867	        if not cid:
  2868	            cid = article_id_for(a.get("url", ""), a.get("source", ""))
  2869	        if cid:
  2870	            ids.append(cid)
  2871	    return ids
  2872

  2874	def main():
  2875	    print("=" * 60)
  2876	    print("  📰 DAILY READS AGENT")
  2877	    print(f"  {datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC')}")
  2878	    print("=" * 60)
  2879	
  2880	    start_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
  2881	    today = start_time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
  2882	    gmail_items: list[dict] = []
  2883	    tier2_items: list[dict] = []
  2884	    articles: list[dict] = []
  2885	    triage_queue: list[dict] = []
  2886	    artifacts_produced: list[str] = []
  2887	
  2888	    try:
  2889	        # Step 1: Gmail scan
  2890	        gmail_items = gmail_scan()
  2891	
  2892	        # Step 2: Tier 2 sources
  2893	        tier2_items = tier2_scan()
  2894	        tier2_items.extend(rss_scan())
  2895	
  2896	        # Step 3: Feedback check
  2897	        feedback_info = feedback_check()
  2898	
  2899	        # Step 3b: Criteria rewrite if enough feedback
  2900	        if feedback_info["should_rewrite"]:
  2901	            all_feedback = load_json("feedback_log.json", [])
  2902	            rewrite_criteria(all_feedback)
  2903	
  2904	        # Step 4: Article selection
  2905	        if not gmail_items and not tier2_items:
  2906	            print("\nNo items from any source. Exiting.")
  2907	            _post_run_heartbeat(
  2908	                start_time=start_time,
  2909	                status="partial",
  2910	                counters=["0 Gmail items", "0 RSS items", "0 articles selected"],
  2911	                artifacts=[],
  2912	                warnings=["No source activity in 7-day window — nothing to select from"],
  2913	            )
  2914	            sys.exit(0)
  2915	
  2916	        # Cross-run dedup: skip candidates delivered in the recent rolling
  2917	        # window so a top pick isn't re-shipped on consecutive days.
  2918	        delivered_state = load_delivered_state()
  2919	        exclude_ids = recently_delivered_ids(delivered_state, today)
  2920	
  2921	        articles = select_articles(gmail_items, tier2_items, feedback_info, exclude_ids)
  2922	        if not articles:
  2923	            print("\nFirst selection attempt failed validation — retrying...")
  2924	            articles = select_articles(gmail_items, tier2_items, feedback_info, exclude_ids)
  2925	        if not articles:
  2926	            print("\nNo valid articles selected after 2 attempts. Exiting.")
  2927	            _post_run_heartbeat(
  2928	                start_time=start_time,
  2929	                status="error",
  2930	                counters=[
  2931	                    f"{len(gmail_items)} Gmail items",
  2932	                    f"{len(tier2_items)} RSS items",
  2933	                    "0 articles selected (validation failed 2x)",
  2934	                ],
  2935	                artifacts=[],
  2936	                warnings=[],
  2937	                error_text="select_articles returned no valid articles after 2 attempts",
  2938	            )
  2939	            sys.exit(1)
  2940	
  2941	        tickers = load_json("tickers.json", {})
  2942	        structured_gmail, structured_tier2 = build_structured_candidates(
  2943	            gmail_items,
  2944	            tier2_items,
  2945	            today,
  2946	            tickers,
  2947	        )
  2948	        triage_queue = build_triage_queue(structured_gmail, structured_tier2, articles)
  2949	        always_read = build_always_read(structured_gmail, articles)
  2950	        journal_watch = build_journal_watch(structured_gmail)
  2951	        substack_items = substack_scan()
  2952	        save_candidate_artifact(today, gmail_items, tier2_items, tickers)
  2953	
  2954	        # Step 4c: Pre-delivery URL liveness check — catch broken links before
  2955	        # they ship. Drops broken URLs from triage/always_read/substack, and for
  2956	        # main slots promotes a live also-considered candidate over a dead link.
  2957	        articles, triage_queue, always_read, substack_items = validate_delivery_urls(
  2958	            articles, triage_queue, always_read, substack_items,
  2959	            exclude_ids=exclude_ids,
  2960	        )
  2961	
  2962	        # The run + triage artifacts are saved AFTER validation, not before
  2963	        # (codex 2026-07-20). enrich_feedback_entry() treats
  2964	        # artifacts/runs/<date>.json as the source of truth for which article
  2965	        # occupied which slot; saving pre-validation meant a substituted slot
  2966	        # recorded the DEAD article, so "slot 1, score 3" feedback trained the
  2967	        # taste model on a piece the reader never saw. Same reasoning for the
  2968	        # triage artifact, which loses its broken + promoted entries here.
  2969	        save_run_artifact(today, gmail_items, tier2_items, articles, feedback_info)
  2970	        save_triage_artifact(today, triage_queue)
  2971	        artifacts_produced.append(str(run_artifact_path(today)))
  2972	
  2973	        # Step 5: Deliver to all channels
  2974	        if os.environ.get("DELIVER_GMAIL_ENABLED", "true").strip().lower() not in ("false", "0", "no", ""):
  2975	            deliver_gmail(articles, triage_queue, always_read, substack_items)

  2978	        deliver_slack(articles, triage_queue, always_read, substack_items, journal_watch)
  2979	        deliver_pages(articles, triage_queue, always_read, substack_items, journal_watch)

  2981	        # reader_pushed record this fills in is persisted by the same write.
  2982	        deliver_reader(articles, always_read, state=delivered_state, today=today)
  2983	        deliver_log(articles)
  2984	        deliver_triage_log(triage_queue)
  2985	        artifacts_produced.append(f"docs/{today}.html")
  2986	
  2987	        # Persist the ids we just delivered so they're excluded from future
  2988	        # runs' selection (bounded rolling window — see project_data). Record

  2995	        print(f"\n{'='*60}")

  2997	        print(f"{'='*60}")
  2998	
  2999	    except SystemExit:

  3009	                counters=[

     1	"""Shared helpers for persisted project state and article identity."""
     2	
     3	import hashlib
     4	import json
     5	import os
     6	from datetime import date, timedelta
     7	from urllib.parse import urlsplit, urlunsplit
     8	
     9	
    10	def load_json(path, default=None):
    11	    try:
    12	        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    13	            return json.load(f)
    14	    except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError):
    15	        return default if default is not None else []
    16	
    17	
    18	def save_json(path, data):
    19	    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    20	        json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

    23	def normalize_url(url: str) -> str:
    24	    """Normalize URLs enough to create stable article identifiers."""
    25	    parsed = urlsplit((url or "").strip())
    26	    path = parsed.path.rstrip("/") or "/"
    27	    return urlunsplit((
    28	        parsed.scheme.lower(),
    29	        parsed.netloc.lower(),
    30	        path,
    31	        parsed.query,
    32	        "",
    33	    ))
    34	
    35	
    36	def article_id_for(url: str, source: str = "") -> str:
    37	    normalized = normalize_url(url)
    38	    seed = f"{source.strip().casefold()}|{normalized}"
    39	    return hashlib.sha1(seed.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
    40	
    41	
    42	def exemplar_id_for(seed: str) -> str:
    43	    return "ex_" + hashlib.sha1(seed.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
    44	
    45	
    46	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47	# Cross-run delivered-article state
    48	#
    49	# The 7-day Gmail scan window (and persistent RSS feeds) re-surface the same
    50	# candidates on consecutive days, so nothing stopped an already-delivered top
    51	# pick from being selected + shipped again the next morning (verified
    52	# 07-10 + 07-11). We persist the ids of recently delivered articles so the
    53	# selector can skip them. The window is bounded on BOTH axes — a rolling day
    54	# window AND a hard id cap — so the state file can never grow unbounded.
    55	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    56	
    57	DELIVERED_STATE_PATH = "delivered_state.json"
    58	DELIVERED_WINDOW_DAYS = 14
    59	DELIVERED_MAX_IDS = 600
    60	
    61	
    62	def load_delivered_state(path: str = DELIVERED_STATE_PATH) -> dict:
    63	    """Load the delivered state: the rolling id ledger AND the permanent
    64	    Reader-push record. Malformed/legacy files degrade to empty rather than
    65	    raising, and a file written before `reader_pushed` existed simply has none.
    66	
    67	    Both keys are rebuilt explicitly. This function returns a NEW dict rather
    68	    than the parsed file, so anything it forgets to carry across is destroyed
    69	    on the next save -- which is exactly how `reader_pushed` would have been
    70	    lost had it been added to the writer alone.
    71	    """
    72	    data = load_json(path, {})
    73	    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    74	        return {"delivered": [], "reader_pushed": {}}
    75	    entries = data.get("delivered")
    76	    if not isinstance(entries, list):
    77	        entries = []
    78	    clean = [
    79	        {"id": e["id"], "date": str(e.get("date", ""))}
    80	        for e in entries
    81	        if isinstance(e, dict) and e.get("id")
    82	    ]
    83	    pushed = data.get("reader_pushed")
    84	    if not isinstance(pushed, dict):
    85	        pushed = {}
    86	    pushed = {str(k): str(v) for k, v in pushed.items() if k}
    87	    return {"delivered": clean, "reader_pushed": pushed}
    88	
    89	
    90	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    91	# Permanent record of what has been pushed to Readwise Reader
    92	#
    93	# SEPARATE FROM THE ROLLING LEDGER ABOVE, AND DELIBERATELY UNBOUNDED.
    94	#
    95	# Reader does NOT dedupe by URL -- measured 2026-09-06 with a controlled probe,
    96	# two saves of one URL return two different document ids. The rolling window
    97	# cannot stand in for that: an always-read source keeps listing the same URL
    98	# (a quarterly letter sits on a fund's page for months), so it ages out of the
    99	# window and would be pushed again.
   100	#
   101	# This is PREVENTIVE. A sweep on the day it was added found no duplicates in the
   102	# library -- the window had held so far. It is here because the window's cover is
   103	# accidental and fails on exactly the long-lived items always-read is made of.
   104	#
   105	# Pruning this by date would reintroduce the bug on exactly the items it
   106	# exists to protect -- the long-lived ones. It grows by at most a handful of
   107	# URLs a day, which is nothing next to the duplicates it prevents.
   108	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   109	
   110	
   111	def reader_already_pushed(state: dict, url: str) -> bool:
   112	    return bool(url) and str(url) in (state.get("reader_pushed") or {})
   113	
   114	
   115	def record_reader_push(state: dict, url: str, today: str) -> dict:
   116	    """Remember that `url` is now in Reader. Never pruned -- see above."""
   117	    if url:
   118	        state.setdefault("reader_pushed", {})[str(url)] = today
   119	    return state
   120	
   121	
   122	def recently_delivered_ids(
   123	    state: dict, today: str, window_days: int = DELIVERED_WINDOW_DAYS
   124	) -> set:
   125	    """Ids delivered within the last ``window_days`` (inclusive). An entry with
   126	    an unparseable date is treated as recent (conservative: don't re-deliver)."""
   127	    try:
   128	        cutoff = date.fromisoformat(today[:10]) - timedelta(days=window_days)
   129	    except ValueError:
   130	        cutoff = None
   131	    ids: set = set()
   132	    for e in state.get("delivered", []):
   133	        try:
   134	            entry_date = date.fromisoformat(str(e.get("date", ""))[:10])
   135	        except ValueError:
   136	            ids.add(e["id"])
   137	            continue
   138	        if cutoff is None or entry_date >= cutoff:
   139	            ids.add(e["id"])
   140	    return ids
   141	
   142	
   143	def record_delivered(
   144	    state: dict,
   145	    ids,
   146	    today: str,
   147	    window_days: int = DELIVERED_WINDOW_DAYS,
   148	    max_ids: int = DELIVERED_MAX_IDS,
   149	) -> dict:
   150	    """Append today's delivered ids, then prune the ledger by BOTH the rolling
   151	    day window and the hard id cap so it stays bounded. Returns the state."""
   152	    entries = list(state.get("delivered", []))
   153	    known = {e["id"] for e in entries}
   154	    for _id in ids:
   155	        if _id and _id not in known:
   156	            entries.append({"id": _id, "date": today})
   157	            known.add(_id)
   158	
   159	    # Prune by day window.
   160	    try:
   161	        cutoff = date.fromisoformat(today[:10]) - timedelta(days=window_days)
   162	    except ValueError:
   163	        cutoff = None
   164	    if cutoff is not None:
   165	        kept = []
   166	        for e in entries:
   167	            try:
   168	                entry_date = date.fromisoformat(str(e.get("date", ""))[:10])
   169	            except ValueError:
   170	                kept.append(e)  # keep undated rather than silently drop
   171	                continue
   172	            if entry_date >= cutoff:
   173	                kept.append(e)
   174	        entries = kept
   175	
   176	    # Bound by id cap (keep the newest by date).
   177	    if len(entries) > max_ids:
   178	        entries.sort(key=lambda e: str(e.get("date", "")))
   179	        entries = entries[-max_ids:]
   180	
   181	    state["delivered"] = entries
   182	    return state
   183	
   184	
   185	def save_delivered_state(state: dict, path: str = DELIVERED_STATE_PATH) -> None:
   186	    save_json(path, state)
   187

   212	
   213	def load_taste_evidence() -> list[dict]:

   215	
   216	
   217	def append_taste_evidence(records: list[dict]) -> int:
   218	    """Append new evidence records, deduplicating by id. Returns count of new records added."""
   219	    evidence = load_taste_evidence()
   220	    existing_ids = {entry.get("id") for entry in evidence if isinstance(entry, dict)}
   221	    added = 0
   222	    for record in records:
   223	        if record.get("id") not in existing_ids:
   224	            evidence.append(record)
   225	            existing_ids.add(record["id"])
   226	            added += 1
   227	    if added:
   228	        save_json(taste_evidence_path(), evidence)
   229	    return added

   232	def _fix_wsl_path(path: str) -> str:
   233	    """Convert WSL /mnt/c/... paths to Windows C:\\... paths."""
   234	    if path.startswith("/mnt/c/"):
   235	        return "C:\\" + path[7:].replace("/", "\\")
   236	    return path

   238	
   239	def migrate_to_taste_evidence() -> None:
   240	    """One-time migration: merge external_exemplars.json + taste_submissions.json into taste_evidence.json."""
   241	    if os.path.exists(taste_evidence_path()):
   242	        return
   243	
   244	    evidence = []
   245	
   246	    for entry in load_json(external_exemplars_path(), []):
   247	        if not isinstance(entry, dict):

   249	        legacy_id = entry.get("id", "")
   250	        seed = legacy_id.replace("ex_", "", 1) if legacy_id.startswith("ex_") else legacy_id
   251	        evidence.append({

   253	            "kind": "positive_exemplar",

   255	            "title": entry.get("title", ""),
   256	            "url": entry.get("url", ""),
   257	            "local_path": _fix_wsl_path(entry.get("local_path", "")),
   258	            "note": entry.get("note", ""),
   259	            "score": None,
   260	            "content_status": entry.get("content_status", "unfetched"),
   261	            "metadata": {**entry.get("metadata", {}), "legacy_id": legacy_id},
   262	            "created_at": entry.get("date_added", ""),
   263	        })
   264	
   265	    for entry in load_json("taste_submissions.json", []):
   266	        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   267	            continue
   268	        evidence.append({
   269	            "id": evidence_id_for(f"github-issue|{entry.get('url', '')}"),

   271	            "source_channel": "github_issue",
   272	            "title": entry.get("headline", ""),
   273	            "url": entry.get("url", ""),
   274	            "local_path": "",
   275	            "note": entry.get("note", ""),
   276	            "score": None,
   277	            "content_status": "unfetched",
   278	            "metadata": {"issue_number": entry.get("issue_number"), "legacy_source": "taste_submissions"},
   279	            "created_at": f"{entry.get('date', '')}T00:00:00Z" if entry.get("date") else "",
   280	        })
   281	
   282	    save_json(taste_evidence_path(), evidence)

   299	    queue = artifact.get("triage_queue", []) if isinstance(artifact, dict) else []

   307	def enrich_feedback_entry(run_date: str, slot: int, channel: str, score: int, note: str = "") -> dict:
   308	    if slot <= 4:
   309	        article = article_lookup_for_run(run_date).get(slot, {})

   318	            "url": article.get("url"),

   330	        "headline": candidate.get("headline"),
   331	        "url": candidate.get("primary_url"),
   332	        "article_source": candidate.get("source_name"),
   333	    }
     1	"""Health heartbeat poster for #status-reports.
     2	
     3	Implements the health/v1 contract from HEALTH_REPORTING.md (§4.1, §7).
     4	Every daily-reads run posts one short Block Kit message to Slack at end of run,
     5	succeeded or failed.
     6	
     7	Spec invariants enforced here:
     8	- Block Kit only ({"text": ...} renders mrkdwn literally on the shared webhook)
     9	- Long error tracebacks split at line boundaries to stay under Slack's 3000-char
    10	  per-section limit
    11	- On Slack POST failure: log full payload, write .health/last_run.json, raise
    12	"""
    13	
    14	from __future__ import annotations
    15	
    16	import json
    17	import os
    18	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    19	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    20	from pathlib import Path
    21	from typing import Literal
    22	
    23	import requests
    24	
    25	WEBHOOK_ENV = "SLACK_WEBHOOK_STATUS_REPORTS"
    26	TAG = "health/v1"
    27	SLACK_SECTION_LIMIT = 3000
    28	HEALTH_DIR = Path(".health")
    29	FALLBACK_PATH = HEALTH_DIR / "last_run.json"
    30	# Touched on every successful heartbeat post. Workflow's if:always()
    31	# fallback step checks for this — if absent, main.py died before posting
    32	# and the fallback step posts a generic error heartbeat.
    33	SENTINEL_PATH = HEALTH_DIR / "posted"
    34	
    35	Status = Literal["ok", "partial", "error"]
    36	
    37	
    38	@dataclass
    39	class Heartbeat:
    40	    project: str
    41	    status: Status
    42	    cycle: str
    43	    start_time: datetime
    44	    end_time: datetime
    45	    next_expected: str
    46	    counters: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    47	    artifacts: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    48	    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    49	    error_text: str = ""
    50	    run_link: str = ""
    51	    attempt: str = "1"
    52	
    53	
    54	def _fmt_utc(dt: datetime) -> str:
    55	    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")
    56	
    57	
    58	def _duration(start: datetime, end: datetime) -> str:
    59	    secs = max(0, int((end - start).total_seconds()))
    60	    if secs < 60:
    61	        return f"{secs}s"
    62	    mins, s = divmod(secs, 60)
    63	    if mins < 60:
    64	        return f"{mins}m" if s == 0 else f"{mins}m{s}s"
    65	    h, m = divmod(mins, 60)
    66	    return f"{h}h{m}m"
    67	
    68	
    69	_STATUS_EMOJI = {"ok": ":white_check_mark:", "partial": ":warning:", "error": ":x:"}
    70	
    71	
    72	def _build_blocks(hb: Heartbeat) -> tuple[list[dict], str]:
    73	    emoji = _STATUS_EMOJI[hb.status]
    74	    duration = _duration(hb.start_time, hb.end_time)
    75	
    76	    header_lines = [
    77	        f"{emoji} *{hb.project} — {hb.status}*  ·  {TAG}",
    78	        f"cycle: {hb.cycle}  ·  attempt: {hb.attempt}",
    79	        f"{_fmt_utc(hb.start_time)} → {_fmt_utc(hb.end_time)} ({duration})",
    80	        f"next expected: {hb.next_expected}",
    81	    ]
    82	    body_parts: list[str] = ["\n".join(header_lines)]
    83	
    84	    if hb.counters:
    85	        body_parts.append("*Counters:* " + " · ".join(hb.counters))
    86	    if hb.artifacts:
    87	        body_parts.append("*Artifacts:*\n" + "\n".join(f"  • {a}" for a in hb.artifacts))
    88	    if hb.warnings:
    89	        body_parts.append("*Warnings:*\n" + "\n".join(f"  • {w}" for w in hb.warnings))
    90	
    91	    main_text = "\n\n".join(body_parts)
    92	    blocks = _split_long_section(main_text)
    93	
    94	    if hb.status != "ok" and hb.error_text:
    95	        err = hb.error_text.strip()
    96	        err_block_text = f"*Error:*\n```\n{err}\n```"
    97	        blocks.extend(_split_long_section(err_block_text))
    98	
    99	    if hb.run_link:
   100	        blocks.append({
   101	            "type": "section",
   102	            "text": {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": hb.run_link},
   103	        })
   104	
   105	    fallback = f"{hb.project} — {hb.status} ({hb.cycle})"
   106	    return blocks, fallback
   107	
   108	
   109	def _split_long_section(text: str) -> list[dict]:
   110	    """Break text into section blocks under Slack's 3000-char per-section cap.
   111	
   112	    Splits at line boundaries so code blocks stay readable.
   113	    """
   114	    if len(text) <= SLACK_SECTION_LIMIT:
   115	        return [{"type": "section", "text": {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": text}}]
   116	
   117	    chunks: list[str] = []
   118	    current = ""
   119	    for line in text.split("\n"):
   120	        candidate = line if not current else current + "\n" + line
   121	        if len(candidate) > SLACK_SECTION_LIMIT and current:
   122	            chunks.append(current)
   123	            current = line
   124	        else:
   125	            current = candidate
   126	    if current:
   127	        chunks.append(current)
   128	    return [{"type": "section", "text": {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": c}} for c in chunks]
   129	
   130	
   131	def _write_fallback(payload: dict) -> None:
   132	    FALLBACK_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   133	    FALLBACK_PATH.write_text(
   134	        json.dumps(payload, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8"
   135	    )
   136	
   137	
   138	def post_health_to_slack(hb: Heartbeat) -> None:
   139	    """Post a heartbeat. On Slack POST failure: log payload, write fallback,
   140	    raise so CI surfaces a missing-heartbeat signal (per §4.7)."""
   141	    blocks, fallback = _build_blocks(hb)
   142	    payload = {"blocks": blocks, "text": fallback}
   143	
   144	    print(f"\n[health/v1] {hb.project} {hb.status} cycle={hb.cycle} attempt={hb.attempt}")
   145	    print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, default=str))
   146	
   147	    webhook_url = os.environ.get(WEBHOOK_ENV)
   148	    if not webhook_url:
   149	        _write_fallback(payload)
   150	        raise RuntimeError(
   151	            f"{WEBHOOK_ENV} not set — wrote payload to {FALLBACK_PATH}"
   152	        )
   153	
   154	    try:
   155	        resp = requests.post(webhook_url, json=payload, timeout=10)
   156	        resp.raise_for_status()
   157	    except Exception as e:
   158	        _write_fallback(payload)
   159	        raise RuntimeError(
   160	            f"Slack heartbeat POST failed: {e} — wrote payload to {FALLBACK_PATH}"
   161	        ) from e
   162	
   163	    HEALTH_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   164	    SENTINEL_PATH.write_text(
   165	        f"{hb.cycle} attempt={hb.attempt} status={hb.status}\n", encoding="utf-8"
   166	    )
     1	"""Thin shim to the shared fleet-wide Block Kit ceiling guard.
     2	
     3	The logic lives ONCE in `<workspace>/_shared/slack_blocks` (a sibling of this repo).
     4	This file only finds + imports it and degrades loudly when it is missing.
     5	
     6	**The degradation is the important part.** A missing guard must never stop a digest
     7	going out — that would turn a safety net into a new single point of failure, which is
     8	strictly worse than the ceiling bug it exists to catch. So `chunk()` falls back to
     9	"send it as one message, exactly as before" and warns once.
    10	
    11	Why this project needs it: `main.py` already calls `_split_oversized_section_blocks`,
    12	which handles Slack's 3000-chars-per-section limit — but nothing checks the
    13	**50-blocks-per-message** limit, which is a different ceiling with the same opaque
    14	`invalid_blocks` error. The digest's block count scales with how many articles were
    15	found, so the payload is largest exactly on the days worth reading. The existing error
    16	handler already prints `(had N blocks)`, which is the tell that this has bitten before.
    17	"""
    18	from __future__ import annotations
    19	
    20	import sys
    21	from pathlib import Path
    22	
    23	_mod = None
    24	_warned = False
    25	
    26	
    27	def _get():
    28	    """Return the VENDORED guard. See `block_ceiling.py` for why it is vendored.
    29	
    30	    This used to insert `<workspace>/_shared/slack_blocks` on `sys.path`. That
    31	    import succeeds on JP's laptop and fails on **every GitHub Actions run** —
    32	    `actions/checkout@v5` fetches this repo only, and `_shared/` is a Dropbox
    33	    sibling. So in CI, which is where this lane actually posts on schedule,
    34	    `_get()` returned None, `problems()` returned `[]` and `chunk()` handed the
    35	    payload back whole: the ceiling guard was inert precisely where it was
    36	    needed, while the fleet recorded this project as covered.
    37	
    38	    Importing a module that ships inside this repo cannot do that.
    39	    """
    40	    global _mod, _warned
    41	    if _mod is not None:
    42	        return _mod
    43	    try:
    44	        import block_ceiling  # vendored, same directory
    45	
    46	        _mod = block_ceiling
    47	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — degrade loudly, never gate
    48	        if not _warned:
    49	            print(f"[WARN] vendored block_ceiling unavailable ({e}); "
    50	                  f"posting unchunked (Slack may reject >50 blocks)", file=sys.stderr)
    51	            _warned = True
    52	    return _mod
    53	
    54	
    55	def problems(blocks: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    56	    """Every Block Kit ceiling this payload breaks. Empty list when fine or unavailable."""
    57	    mod = _get()
    58	    if mod is None:
    59	        return []
    60	    try:
    61	        return mod.validate_blocks(blocks)
    62	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
    63	        print(f"[WARN] slack_blocks.validate_blocks failed ({e})", file=sys.stderr)
    64	        return []
    65	
    66	
    67	def chunk(blocks: list[dict]) -> list[list[dict]]:
    68	    """Split into postable messages. Always returns at least one chunk."""
    69	    mod = _get()
    70	    if mod is None:
    71	        return [blocks]
    72	    try:
    73	        return mod.chunk_blocks(blocks)
    74	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
    75	        print(f"[WARN] slack_blocks.chunk_blocks failed ({e}); posting unchunked",
    76	              file=sys.stderr)
    77	        return [blocks]
    78	
    79	
    80	def to_text(blocks: list[dict]) -> str:
    81	    """Flatten to plain text for a last-resort post. '' when unavailable."""
    82	    mod = _get()
    83	    if mod is None:
    84	        return ""
    85	    try:
    86	        return mod.render_blocks_to_text(blocks)
    87	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
    88	        return ""

    17	    "utm_",
    18	    "fbclid",

    20	    "mc_",

    28	NON_ARTICLE_PATH_PATTERNS = re.compile(
    29	    r"/(unsubscribe|account|profile|preferences|settings|subscribe|login|signup|share|podcast|events?|jobs?|careers?|advertis|privacy|terms)(/|$)",
    30	    re.IGNORECASE,
    31	)

    38	def get_gmail_service():
    39	    """Build Gmail API service from Gmail OAuth credentials.
    40	
    41	    CI path: reads the full JSON from GMAIL_OAUTH_JSON (how the GitHub
    42	    secret is delivered).
    43	
    44	    Local path: reads a file path from GMAIL_OAUTH_JSON_PATH so the user
    45	    doesn't have to paste a 1000+ char JSON into a shell profile.
    46	    """
    47	    from google.auth.transport.requests import Request
    48	    from google.oauth2.credentials import Credentials
    49	    from googleapiclient.discovery import build
    50	
    51	    token_json = os.environ.get("GMAIL_OAUTH_JSON")
    52	    if not token_json:
    53	        path = os.environ.get("GMAIL_OAUTH_JSON_PATH")
    54	        if path:
    55	            with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
    56	                token_json = f.read()
    57	    if not token_json:
    58	        raise RuntimeError(
    59	            "Gmail OAuth not configured. Set GMAIL_OAUTH_JSON (JSON "
    60	            "contents) or GMAIL_OAUTH_JSON_PATH (path to token JSON file)."
    61	        )
    62	    token_data = json.loads(token_json)
    63	    creds = Credentials.from_authorized_user_info(token_data)
    64	    if creds.expired and creds.refresh_token:
    65	        creds.refresh(Request())
    66	    return build("gmail", "v1", credentials=creds)
    67

    69	def clean_url(url: str) -> str:
    70	    """Strip fragments and common tracking params while preserving core article identity."""
    71	    parsed = urlsplit((url or "").strip())
    72	    query_items = [
    73	        (key, value)
    74	        for key, value in parse_qsl(parsed.query, keep_blank_values=True)
    75	        if not key.lower().startswith(TRACKING_QUERY_PREFIXES)
    76	    ]
    77	    path = parsed.path.rstrip("/") or "/"
    78	    return urlunsplit((
    79	        parsed.scheme.lower(),
    80	        parsed.netloc.lower(),
    81	        path,
    82	        urlencode(query_items, doseq=True),
    83	        "",
    84	    ))
    85	
    86	
    87	def is_probable_article_url(url: str) -> bool:
    88	    parsed = urlsplit(url)
    89	    if parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"}:
    90	        return False
    91	    if NON_ARTICLE_HOST_PATTERNS.search(url):
    92	        return False
    93	    if NON_ARTICLE_PATH_PATTERNS.search(parsed.path):
    94	        return False
    95	    if parsed.path in {"", "/"}:
    96	        return False
    97	    return True

   100	_SUBSTACK_REDIRECT_PATTERN = re.compile(
   101	    r"^https?://substack\.com/redirect/2/([A-Za-z0-9_\-\.]+)/?(\?.*)?$",
   102	    re.IGNORECASE,
   103	)
   104	
   105	
   106	def _unwrap_substack_redirect(url: str) -> str | None:
   107	    """Decode a substack.com/redirect/2/<token> wrapper into the canonical post URL.
   108	
   109	    The wrapper is shaped like a JWT (`header.payload.signature` or
   110	    `payload.signature`). The payload segment is URL-safe base64 of a
   111	    JSON blob `{"e": "<real-url>", ...}`. The inner URL is the
   112	    publication's canonical post URL (e.g.
   113	    `mbideepdives.substack.com/p/<slug>`) with tracking query params.
   114	
   115	    Returns the cleaned canonical URL, or None if the input isn't a
   116	    substack redirect wrapper or the payload can't be decoded.
   117	    """
   118	    match = _SUBSTACK_REDIRECT_PATTERN.match(url)
   119	    if not match:
   120	        return None
   121	    token = match.group(1)
   122	    # Try each dot-separated segment as the JSON payload. Substack's
   123	    # wrapper uses `<payload>.<signature>` (2 segments), but fall back
   124	    # to trying each segment to be safe against format drift.
   125	    for segment in token.split("."):
   126	        if not segment:
   127	            continue
   128	        padded = segment + "=" * (-len(segment) % 4)
   129	        try:
   130	            decoded = base64.urlsafe_b64decode(padded)
   131	            payload = json.loads(decoded)
   132	        except Exception:
   133	            continue
   134	        inner = payload.get("e") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None
   135	        if isinstance(inner, str) and inner.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
   136	            return clean_url(inner)
   137	    return None
   138	
   139	
   140	def extract_urls_from_html(html: str) -> list[str]:
   141	    """Extract article URLs from HTML email body."""
   142	    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
   143	    urls = []
   144	    skip_patterns = re.compile(
   145	        r"(unsubscribe|manage.preferences|email-preferences|list-manage|mailchimp"
   146	        r"|click\.\w+\.com/unsub|view\.in\.browser|mailto:"
   147	        # WSJ newsletter "view in browser" wrapper — returns the full email
   148	        # HTML rather than an article. Not a skip by URL pattern elsewhere
   149	        # because the host isn't obviously a view-in-browser host.
   150	        r"|trk\.wsj\.com/view/"
   151	        # Generic CampaignMonitor web-view URLs (WSJ's Ten Point uses these
   152	        # inside Proofpoint). Path shape `/t/<token>/` at a *.cmail20.com or
   153	        # *.createsend.com host is always the email's web copy, not an article.
   154	        r"|cmail\d+\.com/t/"
   155	        r"|createsend\d*\.com/t/"
   156	        # Substack app-link URLs are explicit mobile-app deep links. They
   157	        # land on a generic Substack "open in app" page on desktop. The
   158	        # redirect/2 wrappers in the same email carry the real post URL
   159	        # and get unwrapped below, so we prefer those and drop app-link.
   160	        r"|substack\.com/app-link/)",
   161	        re.IGNORECASE,
   162	    )
   163	    for a in soup.find_all("a", href=True):
   164	        href = a["href"].strip()
   165	        if not href.startswith("http") or skip_patterns.search(href):
   166	            continue
   167	        # Substack redirect wrappers are single-use click tokens — Gmail's
   168	        # link-scanner consumes them before the user clicks, leaving
   169	        # desktop clicks landing on substack.com/app. Decode to the real
   170	        # canonical post URL so the digest works on desktop and mobile.
   171	        unwrapped = _unwrap_substack_redirect(href)
   172	        if unwrapped:
   173	            if unwrapped not in urls:
   174	                urls.append(unwrapped)
   175	            continue
   176	        cleaned = clean_url(href)
   177	        if is_probable_article_url(cleaned) and cleaned not in urls:
   178	            urls.append(cleaned)
   179	    return urls
   180
