    20	def harvest_issues(headers):
    21	    """Fetch open taste issues, parse them, append to taste_evidence.json, close them."""
    22	    resp = requests.get(
    23	        f"{API}/repos/{REPO}/issues",
    24	        headers=headers,
    25	        params={"labels": "taste", "state": "open", "per_page": 100},
    26	        timeout=15,
    27	    )
    28	    resp.raise_for_status()
    29	    issues = resp.json()
    30	
    31	    if not issues:
    32	        print("No taste issues to process.")
    33	        return 0
    34	
    35	    new_records = []
    36	
    37	    for issue in issues:
    38	        title = issue.get("title", "")
    39	        m = TITLE_RE.search(title)
    40	        if not m:
    41	            print(f"  Skipping issue #{issue['number']}: title didn't match pattern")
    42	            continue
    43	
    44	        headline = m.group(1).strip()
    45	        body = issue.get("body") or ""
    46	
    47	        # Extract article URL
    48	        url_match = URL_RE.search(body)
    49	        url = url_match.group(0) if url_match else ""
    50	
    51	        # Extract optional note
    52	        note = ""
    53	        note_match = re.search(r"Why I liked it:\s*(.+)", body, re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)
    54	        if note_match:
    55	            note = note_match.group(1).strip()
    56	
    57	        if not url:
    58	            print(f"  Skipping issue #{issue['number']}: no URL found in body")
    59	        else:
    60	            new_records.append({
    61	                "id": evidence_id_for(f"github-issue|{url}"),
    62	                "kind": "positive_exemplar",
    63	                "source_channel": "github_issue",
    64	                "title": headline,
    65	                "url": url,
    66	                "local_path": "",
    67	                "note": note,
    68	                "score": None,
    69	                "content_status": "unfetched",
    70	                "metadata": {"issue_number": issue["number"]},
    71	                "created_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
    72	            })
    73	            print(f"  Recorded: {headline}")
    74	
    75	        # Close the issue
    76	        requests.patch(
    77	            f"{API}/repos/{REPO}/issues/{issue['number']}",
    78	            headers=headers,
    79	            json={"state": "closed"},
    80	            timeout=10,
    81	        )
    82	
    83	    new_count = append_taste_evidence(new_records)
    84	    print(f"Processed {new_count} new taste submission(s) from {len(issues)} issue(s).")
    85	    return new_count
    86	
    87	
    88	def rebuild_profile():
    89	    """Use Claude with web_search to distill taste_profile.md from all evidence."""
    90	    evidence = load_taste_evidence()
    91	    exemplars = [e for e in evidence if e.get("kind") == "positive_exemplar"]
    92	    if len(exemplars) < 3:
    93	        print(f"Only {len(exemplars)} exemplar(s) — need 3+ to build profile. Skipping.")
    94	        return
    95	
    96	    # Load current profile for context
    97	    current_profile = ""
    98	    try:
    99	        with open("taste_profile.md", "r") as f:
   100	            current_profile = f.read()
   101	    except FileNotFoundError:
   102	        pass
   103	
   104	    exemplar_text = ""
   105	    for i, s in enumerate(exemplars, 1):
   106	        exemplar_text += f"\n{i}. [{s.get('created_at', '')}] \"{s.get('title', '')}\"\n"
   107	        if s.get("url"):
   108	            exemplar_text += f"   URL: {s['url']}\n"
   109	        if s.get("note"):
   110	            exemplar_text += f"   Reader's note: {s['note']}\n"
   111	        excerpt = s.get("metadata", {}).get("extracted_text_preview", "")
   112	        if excerpt and not s.get("note"):
   113	            exemplar_text += f"   Content excerpt: {excerpt[:300]}\n"
   114	
   115	    prompt = f"""You are building a general-purpose content taste profile for a reader.
   116	
   117	EXEMPLAR ARTICLES (articles the reader submitted as positive examples):
   118	{exemplar_text}
   119	
   120	CURRENT PROFILE (if any):
   121	{current_profile}
   122	
   123	INSTRUCTIONS:
   124	1. Use web_search to read/understand each submitted article (search for the URL or headline).
   125	2. Identify recurring themes, preferred depth/style, and valued qualities across all exemplars.
   126	3. Write an updated taste_profile.md with two sections:
   127	
   128	## Preference Summary
   129	### Topics I Gravitate Toward
   130	### Qualities I Value
   131	### What I Tend to Skip
   132	### Source & Style Affinities
   133	
   134	## Exemplar Log
   135	| # | Date | Headline | URL | Note | Themes |
   136	(Include ALL exemplars with extracted themes)
   137	
   138	CRITICAL: This profile must be GENERAL PURPOSE — do not reference daily-reads slots,
   139	newsletter sources, or any specific curation system. This profile should be reusable
   140	for any content curation context (articles, podcasts, videos, etc.).
   141	
   142	Start the file with:
   143	# Taste Profile
   144	> General-purpose content preference model. Last updated: {datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')} ({len(exemplars)} exemplars)
   145	
   146	Output ONLY the markdown content for taste_profile.md."""
   147	
   148	    print("Calling Claude to rebuild taste profile...")
   149	    client = anthropic.Anthropic()
   150	    response = client.messages.create(
   151	        model="claude-sonnet-4-6",
   152	        max_tokens=4096,
   153	        tools=[{"type": "web_search_20250305", "name": "web_search", "max_uses": 10}],
   154	        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
   155	    )
   156	
   157	    # Extract text from response
   158	    profile_text = ""
   159	    for block in response.content:
   160	        if block.type == "text":
   161	            profile_text += block.text
   162	
   163	    if profile_text.strip():
   164	        with open("taste_profile.md", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   165	            f.write(profile_text.strip() + "\n")
   166	        print("taste_profile.md updated.")
   167	    else:
   168	        print("WARNING: No profile text generated by Claude.")
   169	
   170	
   171	def main():
   172	    token = os.environ.get("GITHUB_TOKEN")
   173	    if not token:
   174	        print("No GITHUB_TOKEN set — skipping taste processing")
   175	        return
   176	
   177	    headers = {
   178	        "Authorization": f"token {token}",
   179	        "Accept": "application/vnd.github+json",
   180	    }
   181	
   182	    new_count = harvest_issues(headers)
   183	
   184	    # Only rebuild profile if we got new submissions
   185	    if new_count > 0:
   186	        rebuild_profile()
   187	
   188	    update_learned_preferences()
   189	
   190	
   191	if __name__ == "__main__":
   192	    main()
     1	"""Build structured learned preferences from unified taste evidence."""
     2	
     3	import json
     4	import os
     5	import re
     6	from datetime import datetime, timezone
     7	
     8	import requests
     9	
    10	from project_data import load_json, load_taste_evidence, save_json, taste_evidence_path
    11	
    12	LEARNED_PREFERENCES_JSON_PATH = "learned_preferences.json"
    13	LEARNED_PREFERENCES_MD_PATH = "learned_preferences.md"
    14	TASTE_PROFILE_PATH = "taste_profile.md"
    15	
    16	STRENGTH_THRESHOLDS = {"strong": 3, "moderate": 2, "weak": 1}
    17	
    18	
    19	def _evidence_summary(evidence: list[dict]) -> dict:
    20	    by_kind = {}
    21	    by_channel = {}
    22	    for entry in evidence:
    23	        kind = entry.get("kind", "unknown")
    24	        by_kind[kind] = by_kind.get(kind, 0) + 1
    25	        channel = entry.get("source_channel", "unknown")
    26	        by_channel[channel] = by_channel.get(channel, 0) + 1
    27	    return {"total": len(evidence), "by_kind": by_kind, "by_channel": by_channel}
    28	
    29	
    30	def _strength_for(positive_count: int, negative_count: int) -> str:
    31	    net = positive_count - negative_count
    32	    if net >= STRENGTH_THRESHOLDS["strong"]:
    33	        return "strong"
    34	    if net >= STRENGTH_THRESHOLDS["moderate"]:
    35	        return "moderate"
    36	    if net >= STRENGTH_THRESHOLDS["weak"]:
    37	        return "weak"
    38	    return "weak"
    39	
    40	
    41	def fast_update_preferences() -> dict:
    42	    """Lightweight preference recomputation without calling Claude.
    43	
    44	    Updates evidence counts and adjusts strength levels on existing preferences.
    45	    Cannot discover new topics/sources/styles — only reinforces or weakens existing ones.
    46	    """
    47	    evidence = load_taste_evidence()
    48	    current = load_json(LEARNED_PREFERENCES_JSON_PATH, {})
    49	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    50	    return apply_evidence(current, evidence, now)
    51	
    52	
    53	def apply_evidence(current: dict, evidence: list[dict], now: str) -> dict:
    54	    """Pure core of `fast_update_preferences` — no disk, no clock.
    55	
    56	    Split out so the no-op-run invariant is testable: identical evidence must
    57	    leave the model byte-identical (see the timestamp note below).
    58	    """
    59	    summary = _evidence_summary(evidence)
    60	
    61	    # If there are no existing v2 preferences, build a minimal shell
    62	    if current.get("version") != 2:
    63	        return {
    64	            "version": 2,
    65	            "updated_at": now,
    66	            "topic_preferences": [],
    67	            "source_preferences": [],
    68	            "style_preferences": [],
    69	            "avoid_patterns": [],
    70	            "evidence_summary": summary,
    71	            "score_scale": {"1": "Miss", "2": "Fine", "3": "Strong pick"},
    72	        }
    73	
    74	    # Timestamps are stamped ONLY when something actually changed.
    75	    #
    76	    # Until 2026-07-25 `updated_at` and `last_updated` were rewritten on every
    77	    # run regardless, so two writers (the GH Actions daily run and the local
    78	    # Dropbox taste-ingestion task) produced commits that differed by nothing
    79	    # but the clock -- and then conflicted with each other. That churn built up
    80	    # six local commits carrying zero information; verified by stripping the
    81	    # timestamps, at which point the two sides were byte-identical across all
    82	    # 18 topics. A no-op run should now produce a no-op diff.
    83	    changed = False
    84	
    85	    if current.get("evidence_summary") != summary:
    86	        current["evidence_summary"] = summary
    87	        changed = True
    88	
    89	    # Adjust existing preference strengths based on evidence
    90	    for pref_list_key in ("topic_preferences", "source_preferences", "style_preferences"):
    91	        for pref in current.get(pref_list_key, []):
    92	            name_lower = pref.get("name", "").lower()
    93	            if not name_lower:
    94	                continue
    95	            positive = 0
    96	            negative = 0
    97	            matched_ids = []
    98	            for entry in evidence:
    99	                title = (entry.get("title") or "").lower()
   100	                note = (entry.get("note") or "").lower()
   101	                if name_lower not in title and name_lower not in note:
   102	                    continue
   103	                matched_ids.append(entry.get("id"))
   104	                kind = entry.get("kind", "")
   105	                if kind in ("positive_exemplar", "daily_rating_3"):
   106	                    positive += 1
   107	                elif kind == "daily_rating_1":
   108	                    negative += 1
   109	            if matched_ids:
   110	                strength = _strength_for(positive, negative)
   111	                if (pref.get("strength") != strength
   112	                        or pref.get("evidence_ids") != matched_ids):
   113	                    pref["strength"] = strength
   114	                    pref["evidence_ids"] = matched_ids
   115	                    pref["last_updated"] = now
   116	                    changed = True
   117	
   118	    # Same for avoid patterns
   119	    for pref in current.get("avoid_patterns", []):
   120	        name_lower = pref.get("name", "").lower()
   121	        if not name_lower:
   122	            continue
   123	        matched_ids = []
   124	        for entry in evidence:
   125	            title = (entry.get("title") or "").lower()
   126	            note = (entry.get("note") or "").lower()
   127	            if name_lower in title or name_lower in note:
   128	                matched_ids.append(entry.get("id"))
   129	        if matched_ids and pref.get("evidence_ids") != matched_ids:
   130	            pref["evidence_ids"] = matched_ids
   131	            pref["last_updated"] = now
   132	            changed = True
   133	
   134	    if changed:
   135	        current["updated_at"] = now
   136	
   137	    return current
   138	
   139	
   140	def synthesize_preferences() -> dict:
   141	    """Use Claude to derive structured preferences from all taste evidence.
   142	
   143	    This is the slow path — called when new exemplars arrive or enough new ratings accumulate.
   144	    """
   145	    evidence = load_taste_evidence()
   146	    current = load_json(LEARNED_PREFERENCES_JSON_PATH, {})
   147	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
   148	
   149	    # Build evidence text for Claude
   150	    positive_exemplars = [e for e in evidence if e.get("kind") == "positive_exemplar"]
   151	    strong_picks = [e for e in evidence if e.get("kind") == "daily_rating_3"]
   152	    misses = [e for e in evidence if e.get("kind") == "daily_rating_1"]
   153	
   154	    def format_evidence_block(entries: list[dict], label: str) -> str:
   155	        if not entries:
   156	            return f"\n{label}: (none yet)\n"
   157	        lines = [f"\n{label}:"]
   158	        for e in entries:
   159	            line = f"- [{e.get('source_channel', '?')}] \"{e.get('title', 'Untitled')}\""
   160	            if e.get("note"):
   161	                line += f" — {e['note']}"
   162	            excerpt = e.get("metadata", {}).get("extracted_text_preview", "")
   163	            if excerpt and not e.get("note"):
   164	                line += f" — excerpt: {excerpt[:200]}"
   165	            lines.append(line)
   166	        return "\n".join(lines)
   167	
   168	    evidence_text = ""
   169	    evidence_text += format_evidence_block(positive_exemplars, "POSITIVE EXEMPLARS (reader submitted these as examples of what they want)")
   170	    evidence_text += format_evidence_block(strong_picks, "STRONG PICKS (articles rated 3/3 in daily digest)")
   171	    evidence_text += format_evidence_block(misses, "MISSES (articles rated 1/3 in daily digest)")
   172	
   173	    current_prefs_text = ""
   174	    if current.get("version") == 2:
   175	        current_prefs_text = f"\nCURRENT PREFERENCES (update these, don't start from scratch):\n{json.dumps({k: current.get(k) for k in ('topic_preferences', 'source_preferences', 'style_preferences', 'avoid_patterns')}, indent=2)}\n"
   176	
   177	    prompt = f"""You are analyzing reading taste evidence to derive structured content preferences for an investor-oriented reader.
   178	
   179	{evidence_text}
   180	{current_prefs_text}
   181	INSTRUCTIONS:
   182	1. If URLs are provided, use web_search to understand the articles' content and themes.
   183	2. Identify patterns across all evidence: topics, sources, writing styles, and things to avoid.
   184	3. Return a JSON object with this exact structure:
   185	
   186	{{
   187	  "topic_preferences": [
   188	    {{"name": "short descriptive name", "strength": "strong|moderate|weak", "direction": "positive", "evidence_ids": ["ev_..."]}}
   189	  ],
   190	  "source_preferences": [
   191	    {{"name": "source or publication type", "strength": "strong|moderate|weak", "direction": "positive", "evidence_ids": ["ev_..."]}}
   192	  ],
   193	  "style_preferences": [
   194	    {{"name": "writing style quality", "strength": "strong|moderate|weak", "direction": "positive", "evidence_ids": ["ev_..."]}}
   195	  ],
   196	  "avoid_patterns": [
   197	    {{"name": "pattern to avoid", "strength": "strong|moderate|weak", "direction": "negative", "evidence_ids": ["ev_..."]}}
   198	  ]
   199	}}
   200	
   201	RULES:
   202	- Use evidence_ids from the evidence records (ev_... format) to link preferences to evidence
   203	- strength should reflect how much evidence supports this preference
   204	- Keep names concise (2-5 words)
   205	- Only include preferences with actual evidence — do not speculate
   206	- For avoid_patterns, derive from misses and negative signals only
   207	- Output ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fencing, no explanation"""
   208	
   209	    try:
   210	        import anthropic
   211	        client = anthropic.Anthropic()
   212	        print("Calling Claude to synthesize preferences...")
   213	        response = client.messages.create(
   214	            model="claude-sonnet-4-6",
   215	            max_tokens=4096,
   216	            tools=[{"type": "web_search_20250305", "name": "web_search", "max_uses": 10}],
   217	            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
   218	        )
   219	
   220	        # Extract JSON from response
   221	        response_text = ""
   222	        for block in response.content:
   223	            if block.type == "text":
   224	                response_text += block.text
   225	
   226	        # Parse JSON — handle markdown fencing if Claude adds it despite instructions
   227	        response_text = response_text.strip()
   228	        if response_text.startswith("```"):
   229	            response_text = re.sub(r"^```\w*\n?", "", response_text)
   230	            response_text = re.sub(r"\n?```$", "", response_text)
   231	
   232	        structured = json.loads(response_text)
   233	
   234	        return {
   235	            "version": 2,
   236	            "updated_at": now,
   237	            "topic_preferences": structured.get("topic_preferences", []),
   238	            "source_preferences": structured.get("source_preferences", []),
   239	            "style_preferences": structured.get("style_preferences", []),
   240	            "avoid_patterns": structured.get("avoid_patterns", []),
   241	            "evidence_summary": _evidence_summary(evidence),
   242	            "score_scale": {"1": "Miss", "2": "Fine", "3": "Strong pick"},
   243	            "last_synthesis": now,
   244	        }
   245	
   246	    except Exception as exc:
   247	        print(f"Claude synthesis failed: {exc} — falling back to fast update")
   248	        return fast_update_preferences()
   249	
   250	
   251	def _needs_synthesis(evidence: list[dict], current: dict) -> bool:
   252	    """Check if we should trigger a full Claude synthesis."""
   253	    last_synthesis = current.get("last_synthesis", "")
   254	
   255	    # New exemplars since last synthesis
   256	    exemplars_after = sum(
   257	        1 for e in evidence
   258	        if e.get("kind") == "positive_exemplar" and e.get("created_at", "") > last_synthesis
   259	    )
   260	    if exemplars_after > 0:
   261	        return True
   262	
   263	    # 5+ new daily ratings since last synthesis
   264	    ratings_after = sum(
   265	        1 for e in evidence
   266	        if e.get("kind", "").startswith("daily_rating_") and e.get("created_at", "") > last_synthesis
   267	    )
   268	    if ratings_after >= 5:
   269	        return True
   270	
   271	    return False
   272	
   273	
   274	def render_preferences_markdown(preferences: dict) -> str:
   275	    summary = preferences.get("evidence_summary", {})
   276	    by_kind = summary.get("by_kind", {})
   277	    by_channel = summary.get("by_channel", {})
   278	
   279	    channel_lines = "\n".join(
   280	        f"- {channel}: {count}"
   281	        for channel, count in sorted(by_channel.items())
   282	    ) or "_No evidence channels recorded yet._"
   283	
   284	    def render_pref_list(prefs: list[dict], label: str) -> str:
   285	        if not prefs:
   286	            return f"_{label}: No data yet._"
   287	        lines = []
   288	        for p in prefs:
   289	            name = p.get("name", "unnamed")
   290	            strength = p.get("strength", "?")
   291	            direction = p.get("direction", "positive")
   292	            evidence_count = len(p.get("evidence_ids", []))
   293	            lines.append(f"- **{name}** [{strength}, {direction}] ({evidence_count} evidence points)")
   294	        return "\n".join(lines)
   295	
   296	    return f"""# Learned Preferences
   297	> Structured preference model. Last updated: {preferences.get('updated_at', 'unknown')}
   298	
   299	## Evidence Snapshot
   300	- Total evidence records: {summary.get('total', 0)}
   301	- By kind: {', '.join(f'{k}={v}' for k, v in sorted(by_kind.items())) or 'none'}
   302	
   303	### Evidence Channels
   304	{channel_lines}
   305	
   306	## Topic Preferences
   307	{render_pref_list(preferences.get('topic_preferences', []), 'Topics')}
   308	
   309	## Source Preferences
   310	{render_pref_list(preferences.get('source_preferences', []), 'Sources')}
   311	
   312	## Style Preferences
   313	{render_pref_list(preferences.get('style_preferences', []), 'Styles')}
   314	
   315	## Avoid Patterns
   316	{render_pref_list(preferences.get('avoid_patterns', []), 'Avoid')}
   317	"""
   318	
   319	
   320	def _summarize_changes(old: dict, new: dict) -> list[str]:
   321	    """Produce human-readable bullet points describing what changed between two preference snapshots."""
   322	    lines = []
   323	
   324	    def names(prefs, key):
   325	        return {p.get("name") for p in prefs.get(key, []) if p.get("name")}
   326	
   327	    for key, label in [
   328	        ("topic_preferences", "topic"),
   329	        ("source_preferences", "source"),
   330	        ("style_preferences", "style"),
   331	    ]:
   332	        old_names = names(old, key)
   333	        new_names = names(new, key)
   334	        for name in sorted(new_names - old_names):
   335	            pref = next((p for p in new.get(key, []) if p.get("name") == name), {})
   336	            lines.append(f"New {label} preference: **{name}** [{pref.get('strength', '?')}]")
   337	        for name in sorted(old_names - new_names):
   338	            lines.append(f"Removed {label} preference: {name}")
   339	        for name in sorted(old_names & new_names):
   340	            old_p = next((p for p in old.get(key, []) if p.get("name") == name), {})
   341	            new_p = next((p for p in new.get(key, []) if p.get("name") == name), {})
   342	            if old_p.get("strength") != new_p.get("strength"):
   343	                lines.append(f"{label.title()} **{name}**: {old_p.get('strength')} → {new_p.get('strength')}")
   344	
   345	    old_avoid = names(old, "avoid_patterns")
   346	    new_avoid = names(new, "avoid_patterns")
   347	    for name in sorted(new_avoid - old_avoid):
   348	        lines.append(f"New avoid pattern: **{name}**")
   349	    for name in sorted(old_avoid - new_avoid):
   350	        lines.append(f"Removed avoid pattern: {name}")
   351	
   352	    if not lines:
   353	        lines.append("Preferences refreshed (no structural changes)")
   354	
   355	    return lines
   356	
   357	
   358	def _notify_synthesis(changes: list[str], preferences: dict):
   359	    """Send email and Slack notifications after a taste synthesis."""
   360	    summary = preferences.get("evidence_summary", {})
   361	    change_text = "\n".join(f"• {line}" for line in changes)
   362	    change_html = "".join(f"<li>{line}</li>" for line in changes)
   363	
   364	    subject = f"[ClaudeFin] daily-reads — Taste Preferences Updated — {datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}"
   365	    html = f"""<html><body style="font-family: -apple-system, sans-serif; max-width: 640px; margin: 0 auto; color: #222; padding: 20px;">
   366	<h1>Taste Preferences Updated</h1>
   367	<p>A synthesis run analyzed your taste evidence and updated learned preferences.</p>
   368	<p><strong>Evidence:</strong> {summary.get('total', 0)} records</p>
   369	<p><strong>Changes:</strong></p>
   370	<ul>{change_html}</ul>
   371	<p>Review the full preference state in <code>learned_preferences.md</code> or the repo.</p>
   372	</body></html>"""
   373	
   374	    # Email
   375	    try:
   376	        import base64
   377	        from email.mime.text import MIMEText
   378	        from gmail_reader import get_gmail_service
   379	
   380	        service = get_gmail_service()
   381	        msg = MIMEText(html, "html")
   382	        msg["to"] = "jroypeterson@gmail.com"
   383	        msg["subject"] = subject
   384	        raw = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(msg.as_bytes()).decode()
   385	        service.users().messages().send(userId="me", body={"raw": raw}).execute()
   386	        print("Taste synthesis email notification sent")
   387	    except Exception as e:
   388	        print(f"Taste synthesis email notification failed: {e}")
   389	
   390	    # Slack
   391	    webhook_url = os.environ.get("SLACK_WEBHOOK_STATUS_REPORTS")
   392	    if not webhook_url:
   393	        print("No SLACK_WEBHOOK_STATUS_REPORTS set — skipping taste synthesis Slack notification")
   394	        return
   395	
   396	    blocks = [
   397	        {"type": "header", "text": {"type": "plain_text", "text": "Taste Preferences Updated"}},
   398	        {
   399	            "type": "section",
   400	            "text": {
   401	                "type": "mrkdwn",
   402	                "text": (
   403	                    f"*Evidence:* {summary.get('total', 0)} records\n\n"
   404	                    f"*Changes:*\n{change_text}"
   405	                ),
   406	            },
   407	        },
   408	    ]
   409	
   410	    try:
   411	        resp = requests.post(webhook_url, json={"blocks": blocks}, timeout=10)
   412	        resp.raise_for_status()
   413	        print("Taste synthesis Slack notification sent")
   414	    except Exception as e:
   415	        print(f"Taste synthesis Slack notification failed: {e}")
   416	
   417	
   418	def update_learned_preferences():
   419	    """Main entry point: decide between fast update and full synthesis, then write artifacts."""
   420	    evidence = load_taste_evidence()
   421	    current = load_json(LEARNED_PREFERENCES_JSON_PATH, {})
   422	    did_synthesize = False
   423	
   424	    if _needs_synthesis(evidence, current) and os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"):
   425	        preferences = synthesize_preferences()
   426	        did_synthesize = True
   427	    else:
   428	        preferences = fast_update_preferences()
   429	
   430	    save_json(LEARNED_PREFERENCES_JSON_PATH, preferences)
   431	    with open(LEARNED_PREFERENCES_MD_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   432	        f.write(render_preferences_markdown(preferences).strip() + "\n")
   433	    print("learned_preferences.json and learned_preferences.md updated.")
   434	
   435	    if did_synthesize:
   436	        changes = _summarize_changes(current, preferences)
   437	        _notify_synthesis(changes, preferences)
   438	
   439	
   440	if __name__ == "__main__":
   441	    update_learned_preferences()

     1	"""Reusable Readwise REST client — highlight export (v2 API).
     2	
     3	Fetch-only module, deliberately free of any taste/preference logic so it can
     4	be reused by other consumers (e.g. a future Anki card pipeline). The taste
     5	mapping lives in process_readwise_exemplars.py.
     6	
     7	API: GET https://readwise.io/api/v2/export/ returns highlights grouped by
     8	source document ("book" in Readwise terms — covers articles, books, tweets,
     9	podcasts; the `category` field distinguishes them). Supports incremental sync
    10	via `updatedAfter` (ISO-8601) and pagination via `pageCursor`/`nextPageCursor`.
    11	
    12	Auth: static personal token from https://readwise.io/access_token in the
    13	READWISE_TOKEN env var, sent as header `Authorization: Token <token>` — the
    14	same token main.py's deliver_reader uses for the v3 Reader push.
    15	
    16	Rate limits: the export endpoint is limited (~20 req/min) and returns
    17	429 + Retry-After when exceeded; _get_page honors it with bounded retries.
    18	"""
    19	
    20	import os
    21	import time
    22	
    23	import requests
    24	
    25	EXPORT_URL = "https://readwise.io/api/v2/export/"
    26	DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 30
    27	MAX_RETRIES_PER_PAGE = 3
    28	MAX_RETRY_WAIT_SECONDS = 120
    29	
    30	
    31	class ReadwiseError(Exception):
    32	    """Readwise API request failed after retries."""
    33	
    34	
    35	class ReadwiseAuthError(ReadwiseError):
    36	    """Token missing/rejected (401/403)."""
    37	
    38	
    39	def get_token() -> str | None:
    40	    """Return the READWISE_TOKEN env var, or None if unset/blank."""
    41	    token = (os.environ.get("READWISE_TOKEN") or "").strip()
    42	    return token or None
    43	
    44	
    45	def fetch_export(
    46	    token: str,
    47	    updated_after: str | None = None,
    48	    max_pages: int = 20,
    49	    request_fn=None,
    50	    sleep_fn=time.sleep,
    51	) -> list[dict]:
    52	    """Fetch export records (one dict per source document, each carrying a
    53	    nested `highlights` list).
    54	
    55	    updated_after: ISO-8601 timestamp — only documents whose highlights were
    56	    created/updated after this instant are returned (incremental sync).
    57	    max_pages: hard bound on pagination; exceeding it raises ReadwiseError
    58	    rather than silently returning a partial pull.
    59	    request_fn/sleep_fn: injectable for tests.
    60	    """
    61	    if not token:
    62	        raise ReadwiseAuthError("no Readwise token provided")
    63	    request_fn = request_fn or requests.get
    64	    headers = {"Authorization": f"Token {token}"}
    65	
    66	    results: list[dict] = []
    67	    cursor = None
    68	    for _ in range(max_pages):
    69	        params: dict = {}
    70	        if updated_after:
    71	            params["updatedAfter"] = updated_after
    72	        if cursor:
    73	            params["pageCursor"] = cursor
    74	        page = _get_page(request_fn, headers, params, sleep_fn)
    75	        results.extend(r for r in page.get("results", []) if isinstance(r, dict))
    76	        cursor = page.get("nextPageCursor")
    77	        if not cursor:
    78	            return results
    79	    raise ReadwiseError(
    80	        f"Readwise export pagination did not terminate within {max_pages} pages "
    81	        "— refusing to return a silently-partial pull"
    82	    )
    83	
    84	
    85	def _get_page(request_fn, headers: dict, params: dict, sleep_fn) -> dict:
    86	    """GET one export page with retry/backoff on 429 (honoring Retry-After),
    87	    5xx, and network errors. Raises ReadwiseAuthError on 401/403."""
    88	    delay = 5
    89	    last_error = "unknown"
    90	    for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES_PER_PAGE + 1):
    91	        if attempt:
    92	            sleep_fn(min(delay, MAX_RETRY_WAIT_SECONDS))
    93	            delay *= 2
    94	        try:
    95	            resp = request_fn(EXPORT_URL, headers=headers, params=params, timeout=DEFAULT_TIMEOUT)
    96	        except requests.RequestException as exc:
    97	            last_error = f"network error: {exc}"
    98	            continue
    99	
   100	        if resp.status_code in (401, 403):
   101	            raise ReadwiseAuthError(f"Readwise token rejected (HTTP {resp.status_code})")
   102	        if resp.status_code == 429:
   103	            try:
   104	                retry_after = int(resp.headers.get("Retry-After", "") or 0)
   105	            except (TypeError, ValueError):
   106	                retry_after = 0
   107	            delay = max(delay, retry_after or 30)
   108	            last_error = f"rate limited (429, Retry-After={retry_after or 'n/a'})"
   109	            continue
   110	        if resp.status_code >= 500:
   111	            last_error = f"server error (HTTP {resp.status_code})"
   112	            continue
   113	        if resp.status_code != 200:
   114	            raise ReadwiseError(
   115	                f"Readwise export failed: HTTP {resp.status_code} — {resp.text[:200]}"
   116	            )
   117	        try:
   118	            return resp.json()
   119	        except ValueError as exc:
   120	            last_error = f"invalid JSON response: {exc}"
   121	            continue
   122	
   123	    raise ReadwiseError(
   124	        f"Readwise export page failed after {MAX_RETRIES_PER_PAGE + 1} attempts — {last_error}"
   125	    )
     1	"""Ingest Readwise ARTICLE highlights as positive taste exemplars.
     2	
     3	An article JP took the trouble to highlight in Readwise is a strong positive
     4	taste signal — stronger than a mere click. This step maps recent Readwise
     5	highlights into `taste_evidence.json` as `kind="positive_exemplar"` records,
     6	the exact shape written by the email/Dropbox/GitHub-issue intake paths, so it
     7	slots into the existing loop with no ranker changes:
     8	
     9	    process_readwise_exemplars.py  ──▶  taste_evidence.json
    10	    preference_learning.update_learned_preferences()   (next workflow step;
    11	        any new exemplar triggers a full Claude synthesis)
    12	                                   ──▶  learned_preferences.json
    13	    main.load_learned_preferences_summary()            (ranker prior)
    14	
    15	Scope guard: only `category == "articles"` documents enter the article-taste
    16	loop. Books/tweets/podcasts are intentionally excluded — the loop models
    17	*article* taste, and the standing taste_profile.md prior was already seeded
    18	from the full Readwise book library (see CLAUDE.md).
    19	
    20	State: `readwise_state.json` persists the incremental `updatedAfter` cursor.
    21	It is committed by the daily workflow so CI runs stay incremental. First run
    22	looks back INITIAL_LOOKBACK_DAYS.
    23	
    24	Failure mode: degrade loudly-but-gracefully. A missing/rejected token or API
    25	failure prints a prominent warning AND posts a Block Kit alert to
    26	#status-reports, then exits 0 so the digest still runs without fresh
    27	exemplars. Fetching is done by the reusable readwise_client module.
    28	"""
    29	
    30	import os
    31	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
    32	
    33	import requests
    34	
    35	from project_data import (
    36	    append_taste_evidence,
    37	    evidence_id_for,
    38	    load_json,
    39	    load_taste_evidence,
    40	    save_json,
    41	)
    42	from readwise_client import ReadwiseAuthError, ReadwiseError, fetch_export, get_token
    43	
    44	STATE_PATH = "readwise_state.json"
    45	INITIAL_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 30
    46	# Overlap re-applied to the saved cursor so runner/Readwise clock skew can't
    47	# drop a highlight updated around fetch time; id-dedupe makes overlap harmless.
    48	CURSOR_OVERLAP_MINUTES = 10
    49	# Cap new exemplars per run so one enthusiastic highlighting binge (or the
    50	# first 30-day backfill) doesn't flood the synthesis prompt. Overflow is NOT
    51	# lost: the cursor only advances when nothing was truncated, so the remainder
    52	# drains on subsequent daily runs.
    53	MAX_NEW_EXEMPLARS_PER_RUN = 25
    54	ARTICLE_CATEGORIES = {"articles"}
    55	MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS = 1200  # matches process_exemplar_content.MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS
    56	MAX_NOTE_CHARS = 500  # matches the email intake cap
    57	
    58	
    59	def _now_utc() -> datetime:
    60	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    61	
    62	
    63	def _iso(dt: datetime) -> str:
    64	    return dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    65	
    66	
    67	def load_state() -> dict:
    68	    state = load_json(STATE_PATH, {})
    69	    return state if isinstance(state, dict) else {}
    70	
    71	
    72	def default_updated_after() -> str:
    73	    return _iso(_now_utc() - timedelta(days=INITIAL_LOOKBACK_DAYS))
    74	
    75	
    76	def alarm(message: str) -> None:
    77	    """Loud degradation: print prominently + post to #status-reports."""
    78	    print(f"WARNING: {message}")
    79	    webhook = os.environ.get("SLACK_WEBHOOK_STATUS_REPORTS")
    80	    if not webhook:
    81	        print("  (no SLACK_WEBHOOK_STATUS_REPORTS set — warning is log-only)")
    82	        return
    83	    payload = {
    84	        "blocks": [
    85	            {
    86	                "type": "section",
    87	                "text": {
    88	                    "type": "mrkdwn",
    89	                    "text": f":warning: *daily-reads / Readwise taste ingest* — {message}",
    90	                },
    91	            }
    92	        ],
    93	        "text": f"daily-reads Readwise taste ingest: {message}",
    94	    }
    95	    try:
    96	        requests.post(webhook, json=payload, timeout=15).raise_for_status()
    97	    except requests.RequestException as exc:
    98	        print(f"  (Slack alert failed too: {exc})")

   100	
   101	def _clean_highlights(doc: dict) -> list[dict]:
   102	    highlights = [
   103	        h
   104	        for h in doc.get("highlights", [])
   105	        if isinstance(h, dict) and (h.get("text") or "").strip() and not h.get("is_discard")
   106	    ]
   107	    highlights.sort(key=lambda h: h.get("highlighted_at") or "")
   108	    return highlights
   109	
   110	
   111	def build_exemplar(doc: dict, created_at: str) -> dict | None:
   112	    """Map one Readwise export document (an article + its highlights) to a
   113	    taste-evidence record. Returns None when it shouldn't enter the loop."""
   114	    if (doc.get("category") or "").lower() not in ARTICLE_CATEGORIES:
   115	        return None
   116	    highlights = _clean_highlights(doc)
   117	    if not highlights:
   118	        return None
   119	
   120	    preview = " […] ".join(" ".join(h["text"].split()) for h in highlights)[:MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS]
   121	    notes = "; ".join(
   122	        " ".join(h["note"].split())
   123	        for h in highlights
   124	        if (h.get("note") or "").strip()
   125	    )[:MAX_NOTE_CHARS]
   126	    url = (doc.get("source_url") or doc.get("unique_url") or "").strip()
   127	    title = (doc.get("readable_title") or doc.get("title") or "(untitled)").strip()
   128	
   129	    return {
   130	        "id": evidence_id_for(f"readwise|{doc.get('user_book_id')}"),
   131	        "kind": "positive_exemplar",
   132	        "source_channel": "readwise",
   133	        "title": title,
   134	        "url": url,
   135	        "local_path": "",
   136	        "note": notes,
   137	        "score": None,
   138	        # Highlight text IS the reader-endorsed content — no re-fetch needed.
   139	        "content_status": "extracted" if preview else "unfetched",
   140	        "metadata": {
   141	            "readwise_user_book_id": doc.get("user_book_id"),
   142	            "readwise_category": doc.get("category"),
   143	            "readwise_source": doc.get("source"),
   144	            "author": doc.get("author") or "",
   145	            "highlight_count": len(highlights),
   146	            "latest_highlighted_at": highlights[-1].get("highlighted_at") or "",
   147	            "extracted_text_preview": preview,
   148	            "extracted_at": created_at,
   149	        },
   150	        "created_at": created_at,
   151	    }
   152	
   153	
   154	def ingest(token: str) -> dict:
   155	    """Pull incremental Readwise highlights and append article exemplars.
   156	
   157	    Returns a summary dict (for tests / logging). Raises ReadwiseError /
   158	    ReadwiseAuthError upward — main() converts those to loud-but-graceful.
   159	    """
   160	    state = load_state()
   161	    updated_after = state.get("updated_after") or default_updated_after()
   162	    fetch_start = _now_utc()
   163	    now_iso = _iso(fetch_start)
   164	
   165	    print(f"Fetching Readwise highlights updated after {updated_after} ...")
   166	    docs = fetch_export(token, updated_after=updated_after)
   167	    print(f"  {len(docs)} updated document(s) returned")
   168	
   169	    existing_ids = {
   170	        e.get("id") for e in load_taste_evidence() if isinstance(e, dict)
   171	    }
   172	    candidates = []
   173	    skipped_non_article = 0
   174	    for doc in docs:
   175	        record = build_exemplar(doc, now_iso)
   176	        if record is None:
   177	            skipped_non_article += 1
   178	            continue
   179	        if record["id"] in existing_ids:
   180	            continue
   181	        candidates.append(record)
   182	
   183	    # Newest highlighting activity first; overflow drains on later runs.
   184	    candidates.sort(
   185	        key=lambda r: r["metadata"].get("latest_highlighted_at") or "", reverse=True
   186	    )
   187	    truncated = len(candidates) > MAX_NEW_EXEMPLARS_PER_RUN
   188	    batch = candidates[:MAX_NEW_EXEMPLARS_PER_RUN]
   189	
   190	    added = append_taste_evidence(batch)
   191	    for record in batch:
   192	        print(f"  Recorded exemplar: \"{record['title']}\" ({record['metadata']['highlight_count']} highlights)")
   193	
   194	    if truncated:
   195	        print(
   196	            f"  NOTE: {len(candidates) - len(batch)} exemplar(s) over the per-run cap "
   197	            f"({MAX_NEW_EXEMPLARS_PER_RUN}) — cursor NOT advanced; they drain next run."
   198	        )
   199	    else:
   200	        cursor = _iso(fetch_start - timedelta(minutes=CURSOR_OVERLAP_MINUTES))
   201	        state["updated_after"] = cursor
   202	    state["last_run_at"] = now_iso
   203	    state["last_run_added"] = added
   204	    save_json(STATE_PATH, state)
   205	
   206	    summary = {
   207	        "docs": len(docs),
   208	        "skipped_non_article": skipped_non_article,
   209	        "new_exemplars": added,
   210	        "truncated": truncated,
   211	    }
   212	    print(
   213	        f"Readwise taste ingest: {added} new exemplar(s) "
   214	        f"({skipped_non_article} non-article doc(s) kept out of the article-taste loop)."
   215	    )
   216	    return summary
   217	
   218	
   219	def main():
   220	    print("=" * 60)
   221	    print("  READWISE TASTE EXEMPLARS")
   222	    print("=" * 60)
   223	
   224	    token = get_token()
   225	    if not token:
   226	        alarm(
   227	            "READWISE_TOKEN not set — proceeding WITHOUT Readwise taste exemplars. "
   228	            "Set the `READWISE_TOKEN` GitHub secret (token from "
   229	            "https://readwise.io/access_token)."
   230	        )
   231	        return
   232	
   233	    try:
   234	        ingest(token)
   235	    except ReadwiseAuthError:
   236	        alarm(
   237	            "Readwise token rejected (401/403) — taste ingest skipped. Mint a new "
   238	            "token at https://readwise.io/access_token and update the "
   239	            "`READWISE_TOKEN` GitHub secret (and `daily-reads/.env` locally)."
   240	        )
   241	    except ReadwiseError as exc:
   242	        alarm(f"Readwise export failed — taste ingest skipped this run. {exc}")
   243	
   244	
   245	if __name__ == "__main__":
   246	    main()
     1	"""Harvest local Dropbox taste exemplars into the unified exemplar store."""
     2	
     3	import hashlib
     4	import os
     5	import re
     6	import shutil
     7	from datetime import datetime, timezone
     8	from pathlib import Path
     9	
    10	from gmail_reader import clean_url, is_probable_article_url
    11	from project_data import evidence_id_for, load_json, save_json, taste_evidence_path
    12	
    13	EXEMPLAR_PATH = taste_evidence_path()
    14	DEFAULT_DROPBOX_TASTE_DIR = Path(r"C:\Users\jroyp\Dropbox\Claude Folder\daily-reads-taste-samples")
    15	ARCHIVE_SUBDIR = "Incorporated into taste preferences"
    16	SUPPORTED_TEXT_EXTENSIONS = {".txt", ".md", ".url", ".html", ".htm"}
    17	SUPPORTED_FILE_EXTENSIONS = {".pdf", ".doc", ".docx"}
    18	URL_RE = re.compile(r"https?://\S+", re.IGNORECASE)
    19	
    20	
    21	def configured_dropbox_dir() -> Path | None:
    22	    value = os.environ.get("DROPBOX_TASTE_DIR")
    23	    if value:
    24	        return Path(value).expanduser()
    25	    return DEFAULT_DROPBOX_TASTE_DIR
    26	
    27	
    28	def archive_dir(root: Path) -> Path:
    29	    return root / ARCHIVE_SUBDIR
    30	
    31	
    32	def file_sha1(path: Path) -> str:
    33	    digest = hashlib.sha1()
    34	    with path.open("rb") as handle:
    35	        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(65536), b""):
    36	            digest.update(chunk)
    37	    return digest.hexdigest()
    38	
    39	
    40	def read_text_file(path: Path) -> str:
    41	    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    42	
    43	
    44	def extract_url(text: str) -> str:
    45	    for match in URL_RE.findall(text or ""):
    46	        cleaned = clean_url(match.rstrip(").,>"))
    47	        if is_probable_article_url(cleaned):
    48	            return cleaned
    49	    if text.strip().startswith("[InternetShortcut]"):
    50	        for line in text.splitlines():
    51	            if line.startswith("URL="):
    52	                cleaned = clean_url(line.split("=", 1)[1].strip())
    53	                if is_probable_article_url(cleaned):
    54	                    return cleaned
    55	    return ""

    57	
    58	def read_sidecar_note(path: Path) -> str:
    59	    note_path = Path(str(path) + ".note.txt")
    60	    if not note_path.exists():
    61	        return ""
    62	    return read_text_file(note_path).strip()[:500]
    63	
    64	
    65	def build_exemplar(path: Path, root: Path) -> dict | None:
    66	    relpath = str(path.relative_to(root))
    67	    note = read_sidecar_note(path)
    68	    extension = path.suffix.lower()
    69	    timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    70	
    71	    if extension in SUPPORTED_TEXT_EXTENSIONS:
    72	        text = read_text_file(path)
    73	        url = extract_url(text)
    74	        if url:
    75	            return {
    76	                "id": evidence_id_for(f"dropbox-url|{url}"),
    77	                "kind": "positive_exemplar",
    78	                "source_channel": "dropbox",
    79	                "title": path.stem,
    80	                "url": url,
    81	                "local_path": str(path),
    82	                "note": note,
    83	                "score": None,
    84	                "content_status": "unfetched",
    85	                "metadata": {
    86	                    "dropbox_relpath": relpath,
    87	                    "file_hash": file_sha1(path),
    88	                    "mime_type": "text/plain",
    89	                },
    90	                "created_at": timestamp,
    91	            }
    92	        if not note:
    93	            note = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()[:500]
    94	
    95	    if extension in SUPPORTED_FILE_EXTENSIONS or extension in SUPPORTED_TEXT_EXTENSIONS:
    96	        return {
    97	            "id": evidence_id_for(f"dropbox-file|{file_sha1(path)}"),
    98	            "kind": "positive_exemplar",
    99	            "source_channel": "dropbox",
   100	            "title": path.stem,
   101	            "url": "",
   102	            "local_path": str(path),
   103	            "note": note,
   104	            "score": None,
   105	            "content_status": "local_file_pending",
   106	            "metadata": {
   107	                "dropbox_relpath": relpath,
   108	                "file_hash": file_sha1(path),
   109	                "mime_type": "",
   110	            },
   111	            "created_at": timestamp,
   112	        }
   113	
   114	    return None
   115	
   116	
   117	def archive_processed_file(path: Path, root: Path) -> Path:
   118	    destination_dir = archive_dir(root)
   119	    destination_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   120	    destination = destination_dir / path.name
   121	    counter = 2
   122	    while destination.exists():
   123	        destination = destination_dir / f"{path.stem}-{counter}{path.suffix}"
   124	        counter += 1
   125	    moved_path = Path(shutil.move(str(path), str(destination)))
   126	
   127	    note_path = Path(str(path) + ".note.txt")
   128	    if note_path.exists():
   129	        moved_note = destination_dir / (moved_path.name + ".note.txt")
   130	        note_counter = 2
   131	        while moved_note.exists():
   132	            moved_note = destination_dir / (f"{moved_path.stem}-{note_counter}{moved_path.suffix}.note.txt")
   133	            note_counter += 1
   134	        shutil.move(str(note_path), str(moved_note))
   135	
   136	    return moved_path
   137	
   138	
   139	def main():
   140	    print("=" * 60)
   141	    print("  DROPBOX EXEMPLAR CHECK")
   142	    print("=" * 60)
   143	
   144	    root = configured_dropbox_dir()
   145	    if not root:
   146	        print("No DROPBOX_TASTE_DIR set — skipping Dropbox exemplar ingestion.")
   147	        return
   148	    if not root.exists():
   149	        print(f"Configured Dropbox exemplar directory does not exist: {root}")
   150	        return
   151	
   152	    exemplars = load_json(EXEMPLAR_PATH, [])
   153	    existing_ids = {entry.get("id") for entry in exemplars if isinstance(entry, dict)}
   154	    processed = 0
   155	
   156	    for path in sorted(p for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()):
   157	        if archive_dir(root) in path.parents:
   158	            continue
   159	        if path.name.endswith(".note.txt"):
   160	            continue
   161	        exemplar = build_exemplar(path, root)
   162	        if not exemplar:
   163	            continue
   164	        archived_path = archive_processed_file(path, root)
   165	        exemplar["local_path"] = str(archived_path)
   166	        exemplar["metadata"]["dropbox_relpath"] = str(archived_path.relative_to(root))
   167	        if exemplar["id"] in existing_ids:
   168	            continue
   169	        exemplars.append(exemplar)
   170	        existing_ids.add(exemplar["id"])
   171	        processed += 1
   172	        print(f"  Recorded Dropbox exemplar: {archived_path.name}")
   173	
   174	    save_json(EXEMPLAR_PATH, exemplars)
   175	    print(f"Processed {processed} Dropbox exemplar(s).")
   176	
   177	
   178	if __name__ == "__main__":
   179	    main()
     1	"""Extract content previews from local exemplar files and update exemplar metadata."""
     2	
     3	from datetime import datetime, timezone
     4	from pathlib import Path
     5	
     6	from project_data import load_json, save_json, taste_evidence_path
     7	
     8	EXEMPLAR_PATH = taste_evidence_path()
     9	MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS = 1200
    10	
    11	
    12	def text_preview(text: str, limit: int = MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS) -> str:
    13	    return " ".join((text or "").split())[:limit]
    14	
    15	
    16	def extract_text_from_pdf(path: Path) -> tuple[str, dict]:
    17	    from pypdf import PdfReader
    18	
    19	    reader = PdfReader(str(path))
    20	    page_count = len(reader.pages)
    21	    chunks = []
    22	    for page in reader.pages[:5]:
    23	        page_text = page.extract_text() or ""
    24	        if page_text:
    25	            chunks.append(page_text)
    26	        if sum(len(chunk) for chunk in chunks) >= MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS * 2:
    27	            break
    28	    preview = text_preview("\n".join(chunks))
    29	    return preview, {"page_count": page_count}
    30	
    31	
    32	def extract_text_from_local_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, dict]:
    33	    extension = path.suffix.lower()
    34	    if extension == ".pdf":
    35	        return extract_text_from_pdf(path)
    36	    if extension in {".txt", ".md", ".html", ".htm", ".url"}:
    37	        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    38	        return text_preview(text), {}
    39	    return "", {}
    40	
    41	
    42	def process_exemplar(entry: dict) -> bool:
    43	    local_path = entry.get("local_path")
    44	    if not local_path:
    45	        return False
    46	    content_status = entry.get("content_status", "")
    47	    if content_status not in {"local_file_pending", "email_attachment_pending", "extraction_failed"}:
    48	        return False
    49	
    50	    path = Path(local_path)
    51	    metadata = entry.setdefault("metadata", {})
    52	    if not path.exists():
    53	        entry["content_status"] = "local_file_missing"
    54	        metadata["extraction_error"] = "local file not found"
    55	        return True
    56	
    57	    try:
    58	        preview, extra = extract_text_from_local_file(path)
    59	        if preview:
    60	            metadata["extracted_text_preview"] = preview
    61	            metadata["extracted_text_chars"] = len(preview)
    62	            metadata.update(extra)
    63	            metadata["extracted_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    64	            metadata.pop("extraction_error", None)
    65	            entry["content_status"] = "extracted"
    66	        else:
    67	            entry["content_status"] = "unsupported_local_file"
    68	            metadata["extraction_error"] = f"unsupported extension: {path.suffix.lower()}"
    69	        return True
    70	    except Exception as exc:
    71	        entry["content_status"] = "extraction_failed"
    72	        metadata["extraction_error"] = str(exc)
    73	        return True
    74	
    75	
    76	def main():
    77	    exemplars = load_json(EXEMPLAR_PATH, [])
    78	    updated = 0
    79	    for entry in exemplars:
    80	        if isinstance(entry, dict) and process_exemplar(entry):
    81	            updated += 1
    82	
    83	    save_json(EXEMPLAR_PATH, exemplars)
    84	    print(f"Processed content for {updated} exemplar(s).")
    85	
    86	
    87	if __name__ == "__main__":
    88	    main()

I’m now checking whether any published metrics or scheduling paths create equally severe wrong-output failures.
